- **Combos / meal deals (POS only)** — bundle items at a fixed price (e.g. "Lunch Deal $12"). A combo is a `Product` with `isCombo=true` (price = the fixed bundle price; `loyaltyEnabled`/`stock` forced off) whose makeup lives in new `ComboSlot` + `ComboSlotOption` models: a slot with one option is a fixed item, a slot with several is a **choose-from-group** the cashier resolves at sale time (migration `20260629132222_add_combos`). Combos get their **own dedicated `Combos` category** (auto-created lazily by the combo API, `🍔`/amber), so they appear under a single Combos tab in the POS instead of scattered across food/drink categories — the combo editor therefore has no category picker. Managed from **Manager → Menu** via a new **Combos / Meal Deals** section (`ComboManager` + `ComboForm`; normal products and combos are listed separately). New LAN routes `GET/POST /api/combos` + `PUT/DELETE /api/combos/[id]` (shared shape/slot-build helpers in `src/lib/combos.js`; combo conventions enforced server-side); `GET /api/products` now includes `isCombo` + `comboSlots`/options for the POS builder. On the till, a combo shows a **Combo** badge and opens `ComboBuilderModal` to pick each slot, then adds as **one fixed-price line** — chosen components ride along as price-0 snapshot modifiers (`source:'combo'`, no FK) so they print on the docket/receipt but **don't earn per-component stamps or decrement stock** (single-line by design). Combos are excluded from the public/online menu (`usePublicMenu`) and defensively rejected by `resolveOnlineOrderItems`. All pricing uses the Decimal money framework.

### Changed
- **Decimal money framework is now a mandatory rule** — added a permanent **Money (Decimal — exact, never floats)** section to `AGENTS.md` and `.cursor/rules/dreamycafe.mdc`: all money must compute/compare/store/serialize/display through `src/lib/money.js` (`D`, `roundCents`, `toCents`, `toDb`, `gt/lt/eq`, …) and `formatCurrency`; no raw `+ - * /`/`Math.round`/operator comparisons on money, no new `Float` money columns, client wraps incoming API money with `D(...)`, processors get integer minor units via `toCents`.
- **Money is exact end-to-end (Float → Decimal)** — every monetary value now uses arbitrary-precision decimals instead of IEEE-754 floats, eliminating cent-drift (e.g. `0.1 + 0.2`) across totals, surcharges, discounts, tips, splits, refunds, cash drawer and reports. All 24 money columns are now Postgres `Decimal(10,2)` (migration `20260628120000_money_to_decimal`, casting existing `double precision` → `numeric(10,2)`); the only non-money floats left are `Table.x/y`. New `src/lib/money.js` (`D` safe constructor, `add/sub/mul/div`, `roundCents`, `toCents`/`fromCents`, `cmp/gt/lt/gte/lte/eq`, `sum`, `min/max`, `neg`, `toDb`) is the single math primitive shared by server and client (`decimal.js` is now a direct dependency). Convention: money is computed with decimal.js, persisted as `Decimal`, **serialized to a JSON string** across the API, and rebuilt on the client via `D(...)`; Stripe/Square still receive integer minor units via `toCents()`. Rewrote all arithmetic and comparisons in the server libs (`orderTotals`, `accounting`, `cashSession`, `voucher`, `paymentSettings`, `loyalty`, `onlineCheckout`, `zReport`, `squareReconcile`, `printer`), API routes (`orders`, `orders/online`, `refund`, `terminal/charge`, `cash[/id]`, `reports[/accounting]`), client cart/checkout (`useCart`, `CheckoutModal` + split steps, `pos/page`, `ProductOptionsModal`, `CartItemEditModal`, `OrderAdjustmentModal`, `DenomCounter`, shift/cash pages) and every money display (order/refund modals, public order/track pages, online + sales + reports views). Fixed several latent bugs surfaced by stringified money (string concatenation in revenue sums, lexical comparison of `priceAdjustment`/hourly-peak, `.toFixed` on strings). Verification: `node scripts/test-money.js` (subtotal/surcharge/discount/tip reconcile to the cent, split shares sum exactly, `toCents` integers, lossless JSON round-trip) and `scripts/reconcile-square.js` converted to decimal.js.

### Added
- **Licensing artifacts** — added a proprietary `LICENSE` (all rights reserved; self-hosted use) and a distribution-ready `THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.txt` embedding the full license text of all 160 production dependencies (regenerate via `node scripts/generate-notices.js`). Full transitive audit found only permissive licenses (MIT/Apache-2.0/BSD/ISC/etc.); the only non-permissive runtime component is `sharp`/libvips (LGPL-3.0-or-later, dynamically linked + replaceable, flagged in the notices header). Electron's own Chromium/Node notices are bundled by electron-builder.
- **Recurring reservations** — regulars who come in on set days/weeks can be booked once as a repeating series. New `ReservationSeries` model (`frequency` WEEKLY/MONTHLY, `daysOfWeek`, `intervalWeeks`, `monthlyOrdinal`/`monthlyWeekday`, `anchorDate`, `generatedThrough`) materialises into ordinary `Reservation` rows linked by `seriesId`, so every existing read path (the `/reservations` page, POS Reservations modal, manager analytics, the reminder watcher, and the Reserve badge) picks them up with no changes. Pure recurrence logic in `src/lib/recurrence.js` (`generateOccurrenceDates` handles weekly multi-day / every-N-weeks / monthly nth- or last-weekday with local-time/DST-safe dates; `describeRecurrence` for summaries) with a `node scripts/test-recurrence.js` self-check. DB-aware generation + rolling top-up in `src/lib/reservationSeries.js`: occurrences are pre-generated on a 16-week horizon and topped up automatically at the start of `GET /api/reservations` (no cron). New manager-gated routes `POST /api/reservation-series` (create + generate) and `PATCH`/`DELETE /api/reservation-series/[id]` (edit regenerates only future days; stop removes future days and keeps past ones as standalone history via `onDelete: SetNull`); `GET` lists active series. Shared `ReservationRecurrenceFields` (repeat toggle → Weekly weekday chips + every-N-weeks, or Monthly ordinal + weekday) wired into both the `/reservations` form and the POS `ReservationsModal`; a manager-PIN fallback (`ManagerPinModal`) lets staff authorise series mutations in place. The `/reservations` page gains a collapsible **Recurring bookings** panel (list with `describeRecurrence` summary + Edit/Stop) and occurrences show a small **↻ Repeats** badge. Migration `20260628000000_add_reservation_series`.

### Changed
- **Reservation reminders → Reserve button badge + louder alarm** — reservation reminders now behave like the online-order alert. New shared `ReservationsProvider` (`src/contexts/ReservationsContext.js`, mounted in `src/app/layout.js`) polls today's reservations + settings every 60s and exposes `upcomingCount`/`dueReminders`. The top-nav **Reserve** button shows an **amber count badge** of remaining upcoming reservations (CONFIRMED, still to come today). The reminder chime is **louder** (fuller four-note triangle at higher gain) and now **loops every 5s** while any reminder is unacknowledged, instead of chiming once. `ReservationReminderWatcher` is now just the modal consumer (polling/audio/ack moved into the provider); acknowledging via Got it / View reservations silences the alarm.
- **New online order alert → non-intrusive badge** — replaced the app-wide auto-popping `OnlineOrderWatcher` modal (which a busy cashier could click past and lose) with a persistent badge + alarm. New shared `OnlineOrdersProvider` (`src/contexts/OnlineOrdersContext.js`, mounted in `src/app/layout.js`) polls online orders once and feeds both the nav and the page. The bottom-nav **Online** tab shows per-status colored count pills matching the Online Orders page labels — **red** = new unacknowledged, **orange** = in progress, **green** = ready for pickup (only non-zero counts render). The alarm chime loops every 5s while any new order is unacknowledged and only stops once the cashier opens the **Online** tab and **chooses a prep time** there (PENDING cards have inline prep-time presets/custom that PATCH `{ prepMinutes }` → PREPARING). The **New — Choose Prep Time** section is always visible on the Online page (empty state when none). Removed `OnlineOrderWatcher.js`; the Online page consumes the shared context instead of its own poll.

### Fixed
- **Stop-series popup used the OS dialog** — the recurring-booking Stop confirmation was a native `window.confirm` (rendered in the Windows theme). Replaced with the in-app `usePromptDialog` confirm. Added a permanent rule (`AGENTS.md` / `dreamycafe.mdc`): never use `window.confirm`/`alert`/`prompt`; all popups must be in-app styled dialogs.
- **Reserve badge stuck after status change** — the nav **Reserve** count is driven by `ReservationsContext`, which only re-polled every 60s, so completing/cancelling/no-showing a booking left a stale count until the next poll. The context now exposes `refresh()`, and the `/reservations` page + POS Reservations modal call it after any status change or reservation/series create/stop so the badge updates immediately.
- **Public pages won't scroll** — customer/public pages used `min-h-screen` and relied on window scroll, but the app shell's `<main>` is `overflow-hidden` (so staff panels scroll internally), which clipped content below the fold (most visibly the online-order product grid). All public page roots are now their own scroll containers (`h-full overflow-y-auto`), with sticky headers/tabs preserved: `PublicOnlineOrderPage` (`/order` + `/onlineorder`), `StorefrontHome` (`/`), the loyalty stamp page (`/loyalty`), `PublicTrackOrderPage` (track + its not-found/cancelled/loading states), and `PublicOrderSuccessPage` (success + loading).
- **Stamp card phone lookup** — `/loyalty`, POS `/pos/loyalty`, and checkout stamp-card fields now strip non-digits as you type, format AU numbers, disable Look up until valid, and reject invalid phones server-side on `GET/POST /api/customers` and `GET /api/orders/lookup`.
- **Online checkout phone** — customer phone fields now accept only valid Australian numbers (10 digits, formatted as you type); validated client- and server-side for checkout, loyalty lookup, and order recovery.
- **Admin → Users delete/approve** — user APIs now accept `admin_session` (Owner PIN) as well as `manager_session`; delete no longer silently fails when only the admin cookie is set. Failed delete/approve/decline shows an in-app error message.
- **Double scrollbar on Manager/Admin panels** — body is now a single full-height column (`h-screen overflow-hidden`); only the panel content area scrolls, so the top NavBar stays visible without a second page scrollbar.

### Added
- **POS staff login gate** — the till is no longer open to anyone who merely reaches the shop LAN. Staff operational pages (`/pos`, `/tables`, `/online`, `/orders`, `/reservations`) now require a `pos_session` (any ACTIVE user signed in by PIN) enforced in `src/proxy.js`; missing/expired sessions redirect to a new full-screen `/login` PIN page (`src/app/login/page.js`, lists all active staff, reuses the existing per-user PIN). New `POST/GET/DELETE /api/auth/pos` mints/probes/clears a 12h httpOnly `pos_session` JWT (`signPosJwt`/`verifyPosJwt`/`verifyPosAccess` in `src/lib/auth.js`, scope `pos`). NavBar tracks the POS session and **Lock & sign out** clears all three sessions and returns to `/login`; nav/bottom-nav are hidden on `/login`. Defence-in-depth on top of network security — the LAN data APIs remain LAN-trusted (full per-API auth is a larger follow-up).
- **Reservation reminders (configurable lead time)** — `ReservationReminderWatcher` (mounted app-wide on staff pages via `ConditionalNavBar`) polls today's reservations every 60s and pops a modal + gentle chime for any PENDING/CONFIRMED booking within the lead window. Lead time is shop-wide configurable via new `ReservationSettings` singleton (`reminderLeadMinutes`, default 120, 5–1440): `GET /api/reservation-settings` (LAN-readable, read by the watcher) + `PATCH` (manager/admin), set from **Manager → Reservations** (presets 30m/1h/2h/3h + custom). Acknowledged reminders persist in `localStorage` (`dc_reservation_reminders_ack`, auto-expires ~1h after the booking) so a reload won't re-pop them. Migration `20260626142846_add_reservation_settings`. The **Reserve** button (calendar icon) is back in the top bar (rarely-used, kept out of the bottom tabs).
- **Square reconciliation in Admin → Reports** — the fee/payout check is now a real in-app feature, not just a script: reusable `reconcileSquare()` (`src/lib/squareReconcile.js`), admin-gated `GET /api/reports/square-reconcile`, and a `SquareReconcileCard` on the Owner Reports page (respects the page date range; hidden for Stripe-only shops). Shows recorded card sales vs Square gross, actual processing fees + net, a sales-match badge, mismatches/missing payments, and SENT/PAID payouts with a bank-statement hint. The standalone `scripts/reconcile-square.js` remains for CLI use.
- **Square fee/payout reconciliation script** — `scripts/reconcile-square.js` (read-only) cross-checks `paymentProvider=SQUARE` orders against Square's own `payments.get` records: confirms amounts match to the cent, sums the actual processing fees and net, flags missing/mismatched payments, and lists recent SENT/PAID payouts (money to bank). Run `node --env-file=.env scripts/reconcile-square.js [--from=YYYY-MM-DD --to=YYYY-MM-DD]`. Cash + online Stripe are excluded by design (Square never sees them).

### Changed
- **Reservations: Pending state removed** — bookings are created `CONFIRMED` (schema default + `POST /api/reservations`); the Pending status and Confirm button are gone from the `/reservations` page, POS Reservations modal, and Manager → Reservations analytics. Cards show Complete/No-show/Cancel immediately. Reminder watcher now targets `CONFIRMED` only. Migration `20260627052042_reservation_default_confirmed` flips the column default and backfills any existing `PENDING` rows to `CONFIRMED`.
- **Touch-friendly staff nav** — primary sections (POS, Tables, Online, Orders, Loyalty) moved to a new bottom tab bar (`BottomNav`) with big icon+label targets and safe-area padding; the top bar now shows large labeled utility buttons (Print, Clock, More) and the cramped `⋯` dropdown became a roomy `More` sheet with 56px rows. POS mobile cart bar sits above the bottom bar. Shared `isCustomerPath` (`src/lib/staffPaths.js`) gates both nav wrappers.
- **PWA native shell** — installed till app (iPad Add to Home Screen / Chrome Install) gets fullscreen safe-area padding, no pull-to-refresh bounce, touch-optimised POS; compact staff nav with pill tabs and overflow menu (Manager/Admin); install hint banner when opened in a browser tab.
- **In-app confirm dialogs** — replaced native `confirm()` / `alert()` popups (product/category/user/table delete and error notices) with styled `ConfirmDialog` + `usePromptDialog` hook matching the app UI.
- **Agent rules** — fully merged Ponytail lazy-senior-dev guidance (7-rung ladder, not-lazy-about list, runnable-check rule) into `dreamycafe.mdc` and `AGENTS.md`; `ponytail.mdc` is now a redirect stub.
- **Menu product images** — `+ Add Product` moved into the Products section header; managers can upload JPEG/PNG/WebP/GIF images (stored under `public/uploads/products`) with emoji fallback when no image is set. Images show in manager menu, POS, online ordering, and order views. Migration: `20260624120000_add_product_image_url`.
- **Migration chain fix** — `20260615120000_add_checkout_ref` now conditionally migrates legacy `stripeSessionId` only when that column exists (fixes `prisma migrate dev` shadow DB replay). Added missing `20260624130000_add_order_source` migration so migrations match `schema.prisma`.
- Cursor rules consolidation: merged "simplest-solution-first" and "fix root cause across callers" guidance into `.cursor/rules/dreamycafe.mdc` and made `.cursor/rules/ponytail.mdc` optional (`alwaysApply: false`) to avoid conflicting always-on instructions.
- Canonical rules sync: mirrored the same "simplest solution first" + "fix root causes across callers" guidance in `AGENTS.md` so the canonical source matches active Cursor rules.

### Added
- **Manager → Staff** — managers can request new STAFF/MANAGER accounts (`PENDING` until owner approves in Admin → Users); lists active staff and pending requests.
- **Role-based timesheet editing** — Managers edit STAFF clock times only; Owners (ADMIN) edit STAFF + MANAGER. Shared `TimesheetsEditor`; Owner panel **Timesheets** at `/admin/timesheets` (admin_session). Clock PATCH/DELETE/POST enforce roles; ADMIN manager session no longer blocked.
- **Admin Routine rename** — Owner sidebar **Shift** → **Routine** (`/admin/shift` URL unchanged).

### Fixed
- **Timesheet edit half-hour steps** — edit/add clock-out uses the same ±30 min picker as Clock In/Out (`HalfHourTimePicker`, `clockTime.js`); API rejects non–half-hour timestamps.
- **Payroll shift times** — daily cells show duration plus clock-in/out ranges (e.g. `1:30 PM – 5:00 PM`).

### Added
- **POS customer name at checkout** — optional text field in checkout (alternative to table selection) for regular walk-in customers; stored on `Order.tableNumber` when no table is selected. Dockets, receipts, and sales/reports use `orderLocationLabel()` to show the name (not "Table …").
- **ISP-down cash (Path 3B)** — POS polls `GET /api/health` for local server + WAN (`internet` via cached probe in `src/lib/connectivity.js`). When the shop PC and Postgres are up but internet is down, cash and cash-only split still checkout; card is hidden. `usePosConnectivity` on `/pos`; `CheckoutModal` uses `serverOk` / `internetOk` instead of `navigator.onLine`.

### Fixed
- **Manager Reservations freeze (AU timezone)** — day-range math used UTC `formatDateForApi` after local midnight dates, so `addDays` could stall and `getDaysInRange` looped forever in UTC+10; now uses local calendar dates.
- **Print queue table/name** — failed docket list shows `Table / Name` (table number, walk-in name, or Takeaway) per order.
- **Split card leg button label** — card split payments now show `✓ Charge Card` / `✓ Confirm Order` (with surcharge total) instead of the cash-only `Collected` wording.
- **Public API hardening** — `GET /api/customers` on the public zone returns uniform `{ found }` responses (no `null` enumeration); public `POST /api/customers` removed from the tunnel allowlist and returns 404; customers are created on first successful online payment in `fulfillPaidOrder` (not at checkout or via `/loyalty` registration). `POST /api/vouchers/validate` resolves cart prices from PostgreSQL via `resolveOnlineOrderItems`.
- **Online checkout price tampering** — `POST /api/orders/online` now resolves product, size, and modifier prices from PostgreSQL (`resolveOnlineOrderItems`) instead of trusting client-supplied `unitPrice` values; rejects unavailable products, invalid sizes/modifiers, and insufficient stock.
- **Windows services installer** — tunnel service now passes explicit `--config` and `TUNNEL_ORIGIN_CERT` (fixes cert.pem error when running as SYSTEM); added `fix-tunnel-service.bat`. — `GET /api/health` (public zone) checks app + Postgres for uptime monitors; `scripts/install-windows-services.ps1` + `install-windows-services.bat` (NSSM auto-start); `scripts/restore-drill.ps1` + `register-restore-drill.bat` (quarterly backup restore test); `scripts/bump-sw-cache.js` auto-updates `CACHE_VERSION` on `npm run build`; `scripts/verify-resilience.ps1`; [docs/uptime-monitoring.md](docs/uptime-monitoring.md) (UptimeRobot on `/api/health`).
- **Epson / Generic ESC/POS status check** — after each print on Generic-type printers (including Epson), the app polls standard ESC/POS status for paper out, cover open, and offline/error states; failures go to the print queue.
- **Star Micronics status check** — same post-print poll when printer type is **Star Micronics** (ESC/POS mode); uses Star’s status byte layout on the same DLE EOT commands.
- **Failed docket print queue** — when a FRONT/KITCHEN docket fails, the job is saved to a print queue. Staff see a red printer icon with badge count in the nav bar; open **Print Queue** to reprint or dismiss.

### Changed
- **Codebase refactor pass 2** — completed `orderTotals` adoption (CartItem, CheckoutModal, PublicOnlineOrderPage, `POST /api/orders`); shared `clockPairs.js` (timesheets, payroll, clock-inout); `clientDateRange.js` (Sales, Reports, Reservations); `ORDER_STATUS_CHIP` on tables floor; `usePublicMenu` hook; `currency()` dedup in online checkout; `modifierRoutes.js` factory for product/category modifier APIs; `verifyManagerRoleOnly` for clock PATCH/DELETE; `PanelShell` for manager/admin layouts; CheckoutModal split into `checkout/SplitLegStep`, `CheckoutTotalsPanel`, `CheckoutPaymentSection`, `CheckoutSplitSection`.
- **Codebase refactor (DRY pass)** — shared public order UI (`PublicOnlineOrderPage`, `PublicTrackOrderPage`, `PublicOrderSuccessPage` with `basePath`); centralized API auth helpers in `auth.js`; unified `OrderDetailModal` for Sales + Reports; `terminalClient.runTerminalCharge` for POS + admin test; generic `ModifierListManager`; `orderTotals.js` for line math; unified `ORDER_STATUS_COLORS` / `statusBadgeClass`; removed unused `useOnlineStatus` hook.
- **NSSM optional** — `disable-windows-services.bat` / `enable-windows-services.bat` to switch between `start-pos.bat` (launcher mode) and Windows services without reinstalling.
- **Electron shell-only mode** — when NSSM services already run the server, Electron opens the fullscreen kiosk without a second server or tunnel. Shop PC till: **`open-pos.bat`**. Tray **Close POS window** leaves services running.
- **Shift routine optional** — moved from Manager panel to **Admin → Shift** (`/admin/shift`). Owner toggle turns shift routine on/off; when off, cash sales work without opening the till (no POS warnings or checklist links). When on, owner runs open/close from Admin only; Manager **Cash** shows status and movements without checklist links.
- **Auto-print tax receipt** — applies to all in-store sales (cash, card, split), not only card payments.
- **Multi-brand thermal printers** — **Admin → Printers** per-station **Printer type** (Generic ESC/POS or Star Micronics) and **Paper width** (80 mm / 58 mm). Epson, Bixolon, Citizen, and most LAN thermals use Generic; Star uses a dedicated drawer pin profile.

### Added
- **Tax invoice receipts** — Epson receipts now print as **TAX INVOICE / RECEIPT** with optional business details from `.env` (`RECEIPT_ADDRESS`, `RECEIPT_PHONE`, `RECEIPT_EMAIL`, `RECEIPT_ABN`; name via existing `DOCKET_BRAND_NAME`), GST breakdown, **Prices shown in AUD**, and card last-4 / auth code / reference (Stripe or Square) when the order has a terminal payment. In-store sales can auto-print the receipt after the docket (toggle at **Admin → Printers**).
- **Auto-print tax receipt toggle** — **Admin → Printers** → *Auto-print tax receipt after sale* (`PrinterConfig.autoPrintCardReceipts`, default on). Applies to cash, card, and split.
- **Auto-print docket toggle** — **Admin → Printers** → *Auto-print dockets after sale* (`PrinterConfig.autoPrintDockets`, default on). Reprint docket from order history is unaffected.

### Fixed
- **Admin → Printers save** — `PUT /api/printers/[name]` accepts `admin_session` (Owner PIN), same as print-behaviour settings; no longer requires a separate `manager_session`.
- **Tunnel troubleshooting** — docs warn against `hostname: dreamy-cafe.com/order` in `config.yml` (causes Error 1033); hostname must be `dreamy-cafe.com` only.

### Fixed
- **Marketing / Cloudflare docs** — removed incorrect `order.dreamy-cafe.com` subdomain model. Ordering is **`https://dreamy-cafe.com/order`** (tunnel apex); marketing on **`www.dreamy-cafe.com`** (Pages). [`marketing/config.js`](marketing/config.js), [`docs/marketing-pages.md`](docs/marketing-pages.md), [DEPLOY.md](DEPLOY.md), and related guides updated.

## [4.8.0] — 2026-06-15 — Square Online Checkout (provider-unified)

### Added
- **Square online checkout** — `PaymentSettings.provider` now governs **both** in-person terminal and online orders. Square uses Checkout API Payment Links ([`src/lib/onlineCheckout.js`](src/lib/onlineCheckout.js)); Stripe path unchanged (Checkout Sessions).
- **`POST /api/webhooks/square`** — verifies Square signature (`SQUARE_WEBHOOK_SIGNATURE_KEY`), handles `payment.updated` when `COMPLETED` → `fulfillPaidOrder`. Whitelisted in [`src/proxy.js`](src/proxy.js).
- **`Order.checkoutRef`** — provider-agnostic checkout id (replaces `stripeSessionId`). Migration copies existing Stripe session ids.
- **Online fulfillment stores payment ref** — `fulfillPaidOrder` now sets `paymentIntentId` + `paymentProvider` so online card refunds route through the correct processor.
- **`scripts/test-square-online.js`** — sandbox smoke test for `POST /api/orders/online` with provider=SQUARE.

### Changed
- [`src/app/api/orders/online/route.js`](src/app/api/orders/online/route.js) dispatches checkout creation/verification through `onlineCheckout.js` by active provider.
- Success pages poll `?checkoutRef=` (backward-compatible with `session_id` / `sessionId`).
- Admin → Payments copy updated: provider switch applies to online and in-person; brand blocking noted as Stripe-only.
- [`AGENTS.md`](AGENTS.md), [`DEPLOY.md`](DEPLOY.md), [`docs/go-live-checklist.md`](docs/go-live-checklist.md) — Square webhook setup and Stripe webhook cutover notes.
- Service worker `CACHE_VERSION` → `dc-pos-v3`.

### Notes
- **Brand blocking does not apply on Square online** (Square limitation).
- After switching online to Square, **disable** Stripe `checkout.session.completed` webhook unless still creating Stripe Checkout sessions. Keep Stripe keys if using Stripe Terminal in person.
- New env var: `SQUARE_WEBHOOK_SIGNATURE_KEY`.

## [4.7.1] — 2026-06-15 — Go-Live Operations Docs

### Added

## [4.7.0] — 2026-06-15 — Square as a Switchable In-Person Payment Provider (Stripe stays default)

### Added
- **Provider abstraction for in-person card payments** ([src/lib/paymentProvider.js](src/lib/paymentProvider.js)) — the POS can now charge cards via **Stripe** (default) **or Square**, switchable with one click at [`/admin/terminal`](src/app/admin/terminal/page.js). The POS client (`CheckoutModal`) only ever calls our own `/api/terminal/*` + `/api/orders/*` routes, so the whole switch is server-side and the client is provider-agnostic. Both adapters implement one interface (`charge / getStatus / finalize / cancel / verify / refund`) and return Stripe-style canonical status strings, so the existing poll/finalize flow is unchanged.
- **Square Terminal adapter** ([src/lib/square.js](src/lib/square.js)) using the `square` npm SDK (`SquareClient`/`SquareEnvironment`): `terminal.checkouts.create` → poll `get` → `payments.get` (verify) → `refunds.refundPayment`, and `devices.list` for the admin device picker. Square auto-captures (no auth/void), so brand blocking does **not** apply on Square — it remains a Stripe-only feature (the admin UI notes this).
- **Provider selector + Square device field** on the admin Payments page — pick the active processor (Stripe/Square), enter/auto-list the **Square Device ID** alongside the Stripe Reader ID, and the **$1 test charge** exercises whichever provider is active.
- New env vars (in [.env](.env), gitignored): `SQUARE_ENV` (sandbox|production), `SQUARE_ACCESS_TOKEN`, `SQUARE_LOCATION_ID`, `SQUARE_CURRENCY`, optional `SQUARE_DEVICE_ID`.

### Changed
- **Schema** ([prisma/schema.prisma](prisma/schema.prisma), migration `20260615000000_add_payment_provider`): `PaymentSettings.provider` (`STRIPE` default), `TerminalReader.squareDeviceId`, and **`Order.paymentProvider`** (`STRIPE` default).
- **`/api/terminal/{charge,status,finalize,cancel,readers}`** now dispatch through `getActiveProvider()` instead of calling Stripe directly. Request/response contracts are unchanged (response key stays `paymentIntentId`); the **status route may return an updated `paymentIntentId`** the client adopts — Square swaps its temporary `chk:<checkoutId>` ref for the real `pay:<paymentId>` once the tap completes. `CheckoutModal` ([src/components/pos/CheckoutModal.js](src/components/pos/CheckoutModal.js)) and the admin test-charge poll thread this forward.
- **Refund/verify routing keyed off the order's processor**: `POST /api/orders` stamps `Order.paymentProvider` from the **active** provider and verifies with it; `POST /api/orders/[id]/refund` routes by **`order.paymentProvider`** so a historical order always refunds through the processor that took it — even after the active provider is switched. Online/webhook orders keep the `STRIPE` default (untouched).
- **Agent docs made tool-neutral** — `AGENTS.md` is the canonical guide for all AI agents; `CLAUDE.md` is now a short pointer for tools that load it by convention. Stripe agent skills committed under `skills/` (README/DEPLOY link to `AGENTS.md` instead of `CLAUDE.md`).

### Fixed
- **Admin Payments provider switch** — `PATCH /api/payment-settings` now accepts the Owner `admin_session` (not only `manager_session`), so switching Stripe/Square works after Admin PIN login alone ([src/lib/auth.js](src/lib/auth.js) `verifyAdminAccess`, [src/app/api/payment-settings/route.js](src/app/api/payment-settings/route.js)).
- **Square device picker error** — `GET /api/terminal/readers?square=1` passes `sortOrder: 'DESC'` to the Square SDK (fixes invalid empty `sort_order` query param). Reader list/save also accepts `admin_session` ([src/app/api/terminal/readers/route.js](src/app/api/terminal/readers/route.js)).

### Added
- **Accounting reports for tax / bookkeeping** — Owner panel → **Reports** (`/admin/reports`): date-range accounting summary (net revenue, gross vs refunds, cash/card/split, POS vs online, Stripe/Square card totals, GST ex/incl breakdown). Configurable via `GST_RATE` and `GST_INCLUSIVE` in `.env` (defaults 10% inclusive). **Export summary CSV** and **Export orders CSV** via `GET /api/reports/export` (Admin-only). New [`src/lib/accounting.js`](src/lib/accounting.js), [`GET /api/reports/accounting`](src/app/api/reports/accounting/route.js).
- **Reports gross sales fix** — `GET /api/reports` now counts only `COMPLETED` orders (refunded orders no longer inflate daily revenue) and supports `from`/`to` date ranges.

### Changed
- **Reports moved to Owner panel** — `/admin/reports` (sidebar link). Manager **Sales** keeps History + Online Orders only; `?tab=reports` on `/manager/sales` redirects to `/admin/reports`. Report APIs (`/api/reports`, `/api/reports/accounting`, `/api/reports/export`) require Admin access.

### Notes
- **Scope: in-person only.** Online customer checkout (`/order`) stays on Stripe. Online migration (webhooks/coupons/brand restrictions) is a possible later phase.
- **Square Sandbox setup needed to test**: create a Square sandbox app, set `SQUARE_ACCESS_TOKEN`/`SQUARE_LOCATION_ID`, pair a sandbox device for a `deviceId`, set `SQUARE_ENV=sandbox`, then flip the provider to Square at `/admin/terminal`.
- **Square Sandbox verified (2026-06-15)**: `scripts/test-square-sandbox.js` (direct API) and `scripts/test-square-app-stack.js` (admin $1 charge + POS card order via `/api/terminal/*` and `POST /api/orders`) both PASS. Sandbox uses magic device id `9fa747a2-25ff-48ee-b078-04381f7c828f` — no physical reader pairing in sandbox.
- **Security**: the live Stripe keys (`STRIPE_SECRET_KEY`, `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET`) were exposed during this work — rotate them before going to production.

## [4.6.0] — 2026-06-15 — Automated PostgreSQL Backups + Google Drive Mirror

### Added
- **Nightly database backup script ([scripts/backup-db.ps1](scripts/backup-db.ps1))** — runs `pg_dump` (PostgreSQL 18, found at `C:\PostgreSQL\bin`) against the `dreamycafe` DB in compressed custom format (`-Fc`, restorable with `pg_restore`). Writes timestamped `dreamycafe_<stamp>.dump` files to `backups/` and prunes local copies older than 30 days. The DB password is passed via the `PGPASSWORD` env var (never on the command line).
- **Off-machine mirror to Google Drive** — after each dump the script copies it into `<My Drive>\DreamyCafe-Backups\` (Google Drive for Desktop syncs it to the cloud) and prunes cloud copies older than 90 days. The Drive root auto-detects the "My Drive" mount; if Drive isn't installed/mounted the mirror is **skipped with a warning and the local backup still succeeds**. This addresses the single-machine data risk for the self-hosted deployment (no managed backups).
- **Scheduled daily run** — a Windows Task Scheduler job, **`DreamyCafe DB Backup`**, runs the script daily at 11:30 PM (`-StartWhenAvailable`, so a missed run fires when the PC is next on). Registered and confirmed: a scheduler-triggered run completed with `LastTaskResult = 0`.

### Changed
- **[.gitignore](.gitignore)** now excludes `/backups/` — dumps contain live customer/order data and must never be committed.

### Verified
- **Google Drive for Desktop** installed, signed in to the shop account, and mounted at `G:\My Drive`; a real run mirrored a dump to `G:\My Drive\DreamyCafe-Backups\` and it synced to the cloud.
- **Restore tested end-to-end** — restored the latest dump into a throwaway `restore_test` database and confirmed every table matched live exactly (60 Orders, 83 OrderItems, 23 Customers, 16 Products, 47 ClockRecords, etc.); scratch DB then dropped. Backups are confirmed recoverable.

### Notes
- **Restore for real (into the live DB):** `pg_restore -h localhost -U postgres -d dreamycafe --clean --if-exists <file>.dump`. The `.dump` can come from `backups/` locally or from Google Drive.
- If the DB password is ever rotated, update it in **both** `.env` and `scripts/backup-db.ps1` (the password is hardcoded in the script; the script itself is committed, only `backups/` is gitignored).

## [4.5.2] — 2026-06-15 — Moved Payroll Out of the Manager Panel into Admin

### Changed
- **Payroll is now an Owner page at [`/admin/payroll`](src/app/admin/payroll/page.js)** instead of a PIN-gated tab inside the Manager Timesheets page. It shows up in the Admin sidebar alongside Stock/Users/Printers/Payments, so payroll lives entirely within the admin-token-protected `/admin/*` area.
- **`GET /api/reports/payroll` now verifies the `admin_session` token** ([route.js](src/app/api/reports/payroll/route.js)) rather than checking for an ADMIN role on the manager session — consistent with the rest of `/admin/*`, and it works when an Admin entered via the Admin PIN alone (no manager session needed).
- **The Manager Timesheets page ([/manager/timesheets](src/app/manager/timesheets/page.js)) is now Shifts-only** — view/edit staff clock records. Removed the Payroll tab, its inline Admin-PIN prompt, and the now-unused `PayrollTab`/helpers from that page.

## [4.5.1] — 2026-06-15 — Fixed Panels Re-Prompting for PIN on Every Tab Switch

### Fixed
- **Switching POS → Manager/Admin no longer re-asks for the PIN every time** even though a valid session existed (the Lock button was correctly showing). Root cause: the session-probe `GET /api/auth/{manager,admin}` responses had no cache headers, so the browser's HTTP cache could serve a stale `null` (cached from before login) to a panel layout's one-shot probe on mount — making it think you were logged out and re-prompt. The NavBar masked it because it re-polls every 10s.
  - Added `Cache-Control: no-store, max-age=0` to both auth `GET` routes ([api/auth/manager](src/app/api/auth/manager/route.js), [api/auth/admin](src/app/api/auth/admin/route.js)).
  - Added `cache: 'no-store'` to every client-side auth probe: the [manager](src/app/manager/layout.js) and [admin](src/app/admin/layout.js) layouts, [NavBar](src/components/shared/NavBar.js), [tables](src/app/tables/page.js), [cash](src/app/manager/cash/page.js), and the timesheets Payroll gate ([timesheets](src/app/manager/timesheets/page.js)).
- Bumped the service worker `CACHE_VERSION` to `dc-pos-v2` ([public/sw.js](public/sw.js)) so tablets pick up the new app code instead of serving the stale cached shell.
- Verified end-to-end against a running dev server: Manager and Admin logins now persist across repeated session probes (simulating navigate-away-and-back), and Lock clears the cookie so the next probe returns `null`.

## [4.5.0] — 2026-06-15 — Route Rename (`/manager` + `/admin`) + Fixed Admin-Login Bounce-Back

### Fixed
- **Admin PIN no longer "jumps back to the Manager panel."** Logging in via the navbar **Admin** button showed the PIN modal, but after a correct PIN it landed back on the Manager panel instead of the Owner area. Root cause: the Owner pages were nested at `/admin/owner/*` under the manager-gated `/admin` route, so [src/proxy.js](src/proxy.js) required **both** a `manager_session` and an `admin_session` to load them — but the Admin PIN only mints the `admin_session`. Missing manager session (or the ~5-min admin token lapsing mid-navigation) bounced the user to `/admin/menu`.

### Changed
- **Renamed the panels so URLs match roles** — the Manager panel now lives at **`/manager/*`** and the Admin (Owner) panel at **`/admin/*`** (the `owner` segment is gone). The two are now sibling route trees instead of nested.
  - Moved `menu/cash/reservations/customers/sales/timesheets` + layout/index from `src/app/admin/` → [src/app/manager/](src/app/manager/).
  - Flattened `src/app/admin/owner/{stock,users,printers,terminal}` + layout/index → [src/app/admin/](src/app/admin/).
  - Deleted the now-obsolete redirect stubs (`/admin/{reports,online-orders,payments}` and the old `/admin/{stock,users,printers,terminal}` shims).
- **Decoupled the two sessions.** `/admin/*` now needs **only** the `admin_session` — an Admin PIN by itself grants entry with no prior Manager login, which is what fixes the bounce-back. [src/proxy.js](src/proxy.js) gates `/manager/*` on the `manager_session` (else `/pos?managerRequired=1`) and deliberately does **not** redirect `/admin/*`; the gate is client-side in [src/app/admin/layout.js](src/app/admin/layout.js), which re-prompts the Admin PIN **in place** on a missing/expired token (cancel → `/pos`).
- **Owner links are now Admin-only.** [Sidebar.js](src/components/admin/Sidebar.js) is now a reusable component taking `links` + `heading`; the Manager layout passes the Management links and the Admin layout passes the Owner links — so the **Manager panel no longer shows the Owner section at all**.
- Navbar redirects updated ([NavBar.js](src/components/shared/NavBar.js)): Manager → `/manager/menu`, Admin → `/admin`; button highlights track `/manager` vs `/admin`. New [ManagerSessionContext](src/app/manager/layout.js) for Manager pages; [AdminSessionContext](src/app/admin/layout.js) now carries the admin session for Owner pages. [pos/page.js](src/app/pos/page.js) manager return-url updated to `/manager/*`.

### Security
- Unchanged defense in depth: every `/admin/*` data API stays independently ADMIN-gated, and the public zone allowlist still excludes both `/manager/*` and `/admin/*` (LAN-only). Removing the proxy redirect on `/admin/*` is safe because of those two layers plus the client-side PIN gate.

## [4.4.0] — 2026-06-15 — Separate Owner (Admin) Area + Fixed Manager/Admin Buttons

### Changed
- **Admin (Owner) access no longer persists.** Previously the single 4h `manager_session` cookie held either role, so an elevated Admin session could be left open for a manager/staff to wander into admin screens. Now there's a **separate short-lived `admin_session` token (~5 min)** minted only by an Admin PIN, independent of the manager session. Helpers added in [src/lib/auth.js](src/lib/auth.js) (`ADMIN_COOKIE`, `signAdminJwt`, `verifyAdminJwt` — scoped+role-checked), new auth route [src/app/api/auth/admin/route.js](src/app/api/auth/admin/route.js).
- **Admin-only pages moved to `/admin/owner/*`** — Payments, Printers, Users, Stock relocated under [src/app/admin/owner/](src/app/admin/owner/) with redirect stubs left at the old `/admin/{terminal,printers,users,stock}` paths. New [owner/layout.js](src/app/admin/owner/layout.js) re-prompts for an Admin PIN (reusing `ManagerPinModal`) whenever the admin token is missing/expired. [src/proxy.js](src/proxy.js) now requires a valid `admin_session` for `/admin/owner/*` (else bounces to `/admin/menu?adminRequired=1`); manager pages still need only the manager session.
- **Payroll tab** (in `/admin/timesheets`) is now gated by the admin token too — clicking it re-prompts for an Admin PIN rather than keying off the manager session role.
- **Fixed the Manager/Admin navbar buttons** ([src/components/shared/NavBar.js](src/components/shared/NavBar.js)): they previously became near-no-ops once any session existed. Now **Manager always prompts the Manager PIN → `/admin/menu`** and **Admin always prompts the Admin PIN → `/admin/owner`**. The **Lock** button now clears **both** the manager session and the admin token.
- [Sidebar.js](src/components/admin/Sidebar.js) split into a **Management** group and an **Owner 🔒** group (owner links point at `/admin/owner/*`). [ManagerPinModal](src/components/shared/ManagerPinModal.js) gained an `endpoint` prop so the Admin instance posts to `/api/auth/admin`.

### Security
- Per-API ADMIN 403s are unchanged (defense in depth). This adds the session/route/UX layer: a manager session alone can never open `/admin/owner/*`, and admin power self-expires so it's never left logged in.

## [4.3.1] — 2026-06-15 — Launchers No Longer Auto-Open a Browser

### Changed
- `start-pos.bat` and `start-pos-dev.bat` no longer run `start "" "http://localhost:3000"` at the end, so they stop opening a browser tab on launch. They still start the server + Cloudflare tunnel; the POS is now opened from the installed PWA app icon instead. (Also dropped the now-pointless `timeout` waits before that line.)

## [4.3.0] — 2026-06-14 — Single POS Till Window (Same-Device Lock)

### Added
- **Only one POS till window can be active per device.** Opening `/pos` a second time in the same browser/PWA now shows a 🔒 "POS is open in another window" screen with a **"Use the POS here"** takeover button (the other window then yields). Prevents accidentally ringing orders in two windows on one machine. New hook [src/hooks/usePosLock.js](src/hooks/usePosLock.js) (BroadcastChannel `dc-pos-lock`: probe-on-open, answer-if-owner, takeover, release-on-close); lock screen + early return wired into [src/app/pos/page.js](src/app/pos/page.js).
- **Deliberately narrow scope:** the guard runs only on `/pos`, so the Manager/Admin view, Tables, Online tab, and customer pages on **other devices keep working** — a manager on a separate phone/tablet is never blocked. It's same-browser/same-device only (BroadcastChannel can't see other devices/profiles), which exactly matches the "don't open the till twice on one machine" goal.

## [4.2.0] — 2026-06-14 — Offline-Tolerant POS (Installable PWA)

The staff POS now survives a network drop for **browsing and order-building** (not payment), and is installable to the tablet home screen. This is Level A only — there is **no offline card charging or offline order saving** (server/Stripe-driven; Stripe offers no store-and-forward to custom integrations).

### Added
- **Service worker** ([public/sw.js](public/sw.js)) — hand-written, no new deps. Caches the app shell + static assets (cache-first w/ revalidate for navigations & `_next/static`); **never caches `/api/*`** so orders/cash/terminal/menu stay fresh online. Versioned via `CACHE_VERSION` (bump on each deploy that changes app code; old caches purged on activate). Registered only on staff/LAN pages by [ServiceWorkerRegister.js](src/components/shared/ServiceWorkerRegister.js) (mounted in [ConditionalNavBar.js](src/components/shared/ConditionalNavBar.js), absent on `/order*` + `/onlineorder*`), which also reloads once when a new SW activates so deploys aren't stuck behind a stale shell.
- **Installable PWA** — [public/manifest.json](public/manifest.json) (+ `public/icon.svg`, `public/offline.html`) and `manifest`/`appleWebApp`/`themeColor` metadata in [layout.js](src/app/layout.js). `start_url: /pos`, standalone display.
- **Offline menu fallback** — [useMenu.js](src/hooks/useMenu.js) now caches the last good `/api/categories` + `/api/products` to `localStorage` (`dc_menu_cache`) and serves it when the network is unreachable, returning a `fromCache` flag. The cart ([useCart.js](src/hooks/useCart.js)) was already pure client-side, so order-building works offline too.
- **Connectivity UX + checkout guard** — new [useOnlineStatus.js](src/hooks/useOnlineStatus.js); the POS shows an amber "Offline — reconnect to take payment" banner (and a "last saved menu" note while reconnecting), and [CheckoutModal.js](src/components/pos/CheckoutModal.js) disables all payment actions while offline (folded into `canConfirm`), with a hard guard in `handleConfirmOrder` so an order is never submitted offline (no failed/duplicate writes).

### Changed
- [src/proxy.js](src/proxy.js) middleware `matcher` now also excludes `sw.js`, `manifest.json`, `offline.html`, `icon.svg` so the PWA static files are served untouched (and never caught by the public-zone redirect).

### Notes
- Offline menu is the *last cached* copy — an admin price change won't reach an offline tablet until it reconnects. The PNG manifest icons were omitted (SVG-only) — add `icon-192.png`/`icon-512.png` later if you want richer install icons on some platforms.

## [4.1.0] — 2026-06-13 — Customer Order-Tracking Recovery

### Added
- **Customers can re-open their tracking page after closing it.** The tracking URL holds an unguessable `trackingToken` that was previously only in the browser tab — close it and the link was lost. Two recovery paths now exist on the customer order pages ([onlineorder/page.js](src/app/onlineorder/page.js), [order/page.js](src/app/order/page.js)):
  - **Track my recent order** — the track pages now save the active order's link to `localStorage` (`dc_last_order`, cleared when the order is COMPLETED/CANCELLED); a button reopens it. Same-device, zero friction.
  - **Find my order by phone** — re-enter the checkout phone to retrieve the active order's tracking link, via new **`GET /api/orders/lookup?phone=`** ([src/app/api/orders/lookup/route.js](src/app/api/orders/lookup/route.js)). Rate-limited (10/min/IP, reuses `src/lib/ratelimit.js`), returns only the `trackingToken`+status of the customer's most recent **active** online order (PENDING/PREPARING/READY) — never the id, other customers' orders, or the raw note. Allowlisted for the public zone in [src/proxy.js](src/proxy.js).

## [4.0.0] — 2026-06-13 — In-Store Workflow Removed; Online Live Tab + New-Order Alert

Reworked the order model to match how the shop actually runs: in-store orders are made-and-carried (docket only), and the PENDING→PREPARING→READY→COMPLETED lifecycle now applies **only to online pickup orders**.

### Changed
- **In-store POS orders are created `COMPLETED`** ([src/app/api/orders/route.js](src/app/api/orders/route.js)) instead of `PENDING`. The docket still prints on checkout; staff make it and run it to the table — no status buttons. Sales/cash/reports already count all non-CANCELLED orders, so revenue is unaffected; refunds still work (and still require COMPLETED, which now applies immediately).
- **Nav**: removed Kitchen and Front; added **Online**. New order: POS · Tables · Online · Orders · Loyalty ([NavBar.js](src/components/shared/NavBar.js)). LAN allowlist in [src/proxy.js](src/proxy.js) updated to `pos|tables|online|orders`.

### Added
- **`/online` live tab** ([src/app/online/page.js](src/app/online/page.js)) — polls paid online orders every 10s, split into **In Progress** (PENDING/PREPARING, shows the prep estimate) and **Ready for Pickup** (READY). **Mark Ready** → READY; **Collected** → COMPLETED (drops out of the queue). Replaces the Kitchen/Front displays for the only channel that still needs a queue.
- **New-online-order notification** ([src/components/shared/OnlineOrderWatcher.js](src/components/shared/OnlineOrderWatcher.js), mounted app-wide via [ConditionalNavBar.js](src/components/shared/ConditionalNavBar.js)) — polls for new paid online orders and, on the staff tablet (any staff page, never customer pages), pops a modal with a Web-Audio chime. The chime is a loud rising square-wave pattern that **repeats every 5s while any new order is still unacknowledged**, so a busy shop doesn't miss it; it stops as soon as the alert is acknowledged (prep time set) or dismissed. Audio uses a single shared `AudioContext` resumed on the first user gesture (the earlier per-beep context creation only sounded once and never repeated, because browsers leave fresh contexts suspended/capped). Staff pick a prep time (5/10/15/20/30 or custom); this saves `prepMinutes` and advances the order to **PREPARING**. Seeds its seen-set on first load so it never re-alerts existing orders; queues multiple arrivals; "Dismiss" defers to the Online tab.
- **Prep-time estimate for customers** — new nullable `Order.prepMinutes` (migration `20260613120000_add_order_prep_minutes`). Returned by the public track API ([track/[id]/route.js](src/app/api/orders/track/[id]/route.js)) and shown as a static "Estimated prep time: X min" on both customer tracking pages ([onlineorder/track/[id]](src/app/onlineorder/track/[id]/page.js), [order/track/[id]](src/app/order/track/[id]/page.js)).

### Removed
- **Edit Order** everywhere — to change an order, staff refund it and ring a new one. Deleted `EditOrderModal.js`; removed the Edit button + state from [OrderHistoryTable.js](src/components/admin/reports/OrderHistoryTable.js); removed the item-rebuild branch from `PATCH /api/orders/[id]` (the route now only sets status / prepMinutes).
- **Kitchen & Front displays** — deleted `src/app/kitchen`, `src/app/front`, `src/components/kitchen/OrderCard.js`, and the now-unused `src/hooks/useOrders.js`.

### Notes
- `PATCH /api/orders/[id]` now accepts `{ prepMinutes }` (sets PREPARING) and/or `{ status }`; the CANCELLED→free-table logic is unchanged.

## [3.9.0] — 2026-06-13 — Cash Refunds Adjust the Drawer

### Fixed
- **Refunding a cash order now removes the cash from the till.** Previously a refund only set `status = 'REFUNDED'` and (for card/online) issued a Stripe refund — it never touched the cash drawer, yet the open session's `expectedCash` still counted the refunded order's total (the cash-sales sum excludes only `CANCELLED`). So after a cash refund the drawer was physically short by the refund amount but the system didn't know, producing an unexplained negative variance at close-out with no audit trail.

### Changed
- [src/app/api/orders/[id]/refund/route.js](src/app/api/orders/[id]/refund/route.js): on refund, when cash was involved, log a `CashMovement` of `type: 'OUT'` (note `Refund order #N`, `userId` = the refunding manager) against the current open `CashSession`, inside the same `prisma.$transaction` as the status update. **CASH** → out the full `order.total`; **SPLIT** → out the cashier-entered "cash returned" amount (validated `0 ≤ x ≤ total`); **CARD/ONLINE** → no movement (Stripe only). The existing `expectedCash` formula already subtracts `totalOut`, so the expected till now drops automatically and the deduction shows in the Cash Movements table.
- [src/components/admin/sales/RefundModal.js](src/components/admin/sales/RefundModal.js): CASH refunds show a note that the total will leave the drawer; **SPLIT** refunds prompt for "Cash returned from drawer" (defaulted to the total; cashier reduces it by the card portion) and send it as `cashReturned`. Card legs still auto-refund via Stripe.

## [3.8.2] — 2026-06-13 — Fix: Refund from Order History Crashed the List

### Fixed
- **Refunding from the Order History tab threw `Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'map')`.** The refund API (`POST /api/orders/[id]/refund`) returns only the changed fields (`status`, `refundNote`) with no `items` relation, but [src/app/orders/page.js](src/app/orders/page.js) `handleOrderUpdated` **replaced** the list order with that partial response, dropping `items` — so the row's `order.items.map(...)` crashed on re-render. Now it **merges** (`{ ...o, ...updated }`), matching what [SalesHistoryView.js](src/components/admin/sales/SalesHistoryView.js) already did. Also hardened both `order.items.map` sites in [OrderHistoryTable.js](src/components/admin/reports/OrderHistoryTable.js) with `(order.items ?? [])` as defense-in-depth.

## [3.8.1] — 2026-06-13 — Admin Order Lists Show Item Names

### Changed
- **Admin order list rows now show item names instead of emojis** in the **Order History**, **Sales History**, and **Online Orders** tabs. Each row's Items column previously rendered just the product emoji (e.g. `🥐×1`), which is tiny and ambiguous since several products share an emoji — staff could misread one item for another. Rows now read `Name ×qty`, comma-separated, with no cap (long orders wrap in the cell). Files: [OrderHistoryTable.js](src/components/admin/reports/OrderHistoryTable.js), [SalesHistoryView.js](src/components/admin/sales/SalesHistoryView.js) (dropped the old slice-to-3 `+N more`), [OnlineOrdersView.js](src/components/admin/online/OnlineOrdersView.js). Detail modals were already showing full names and are unchanged.

## [3.8.0] — 2026-06-13 — Split Bill: By Price + By Product (Lightspeed-style)

### Changed
- **Reworked the POS Split-bill flow** in [src/components/pos/CheckoutModal.js](src/components/pos/CheckoutModal.js) from a fixed "split equally between N people" model into two Lightspeed-style modes, chosen via a tab at the top of the split panel (locked once the first payment is collected):
  - **Split by price** — the cashier enters any amount for each payment leg (defaults to the remaining balance), with quick `÷2 / ÷3 / ÷4` buttons that fill the input with an equal share of what's left and a "Remaining" button. Legs repeat until the full total is collected.
  - **Split by product** — the cashier taps line items into the current payment leg, charges it, then assigns the remaining items to the next leg until every line is paid. Each line carries its proportional share of `grandTotal` (so order-level surcharge/discount/tip/loyalty are spread across items and all legs still sum to the order total; last leg absorbs rounding).
- Each leg still collects cash **or** card independently via the shared `runTerminalCharge` helper; card legs charge the reader for their share **incl. the flat card surcharge** and brand enforcement, exactly as before. The order records the aggregate `amountPaid`/`change`, comma-joined card-leg PaymentIntents, and `paymentMethod` = the single method if uniform else `SPLIT`.
- Generalized the former `SplitPersonStep` into `SplitLegStep` (shows the leg's items when splitting by product) and replaced the `splitCount`/per-person state with `splitType` + per-leg amount/item selection state.

### Verified
- `next build` compiles successfully. Needs a manual on-tablet pass: by-price custom + ÷N + remaining legs reaching $0; by-product tap-assign across legs; mixed cash/card legs with the surcharge; card legs against the real reader.

## [3.7.1] — 2026-06-13 — Tables: Side-by-Side Groups + Floor Redesign

### Changed
- **Grouped tables auto-reflow into a horizontal row** — previously grouped tables stayed at whatever overlapping positions they were dropped on, so the circles stacked on top of each other. On confirming a group, members now snap into a single edge-to-edge horizontal row (new `rowLayout` helper in [src/app/tables/page.js](src/app/tables/page.js)), anchored at the row's leftmost member and clamped to stay on the floor. Positions are persisted via `PATCH /api/tables/[id]`; merging into an existing group re-lays out the whole group
- **Redesigned Floor Plan background** — replaced the flat beige fill + single dot grid with a warm cafe look: a soft cream→latte radial gradient base, a softened dot grid plus a faint large-circle "weave" texture for depth, and a gentle radial vignette framing the floor. All decorative layers are `pointer-events-none` so table drag/select is unaffected

## [3.7.0] — 2026-06-13 — Tables Tab Overhaul

### Changed
- **Touch + drag-threshold floor plan** — table dragging now uses Pointer Events (works on touch screens, `touch-action: none`, pointer capture) with a ~6px threshold so a tap cleanly selects and a drag cleanly moves; the old select-after-drag glitch is gone
- **Layout lock** — new "🔒 Layout Locked / ✏️ Editing Layout" toggle in the floor-plan header; tables can only be dragged (and grouped) in edit mode, so taps during service never nudge the layout
- **Table delete is MANAGER/ADMIN-only** — `DELETE /api/tables/[id]` was previously unauthenticated; it now requires a manager session (403 otherwise). The Tables page prompts with the Manager PIN modal when no session is active

### Added
- **Table grouping with shared occupancy** — `Table.groupId` (migration `20260613000000_add_table_group`). In edit mode, dropping a table within ~120px of another offers "Group?"; grouped tables move as one, show a colored ring + ⛓ chip, and seat one party: an order on any member occupies the whole group, freeing/cancelling frees the whole group (only when no member has an active order). Ungroup from the side panel. New `POST/DELETE /api/tables/group`; merging into an existing group reuses its id
- **`src/lib/tables.js` → `setTableStatus(tableId, status)`** — group-aware status helper, now used by order creation, order cancellation, and the tables PATCH route. Always use it instead of updating `Table.status` directly
- **Edit table label** — inline label editing in the side panel (field existed in the DB but had no UI)
- **Auto-refresh** — the floor reloads every 10s (paused while dragging or the group prompt is open) so occupancy changes from other devices appear live
- **New Order from table** — side-panel button jumps to `/pos?table=<id>`; `CheckoutModal` accepts `initialTableId` and preselects that table

### Verified
- API tests: unauthenticated DELETE → 403; group → shared `groupId`; PATCH one member OCCUPIED → both OCCUPIED; free one → both AVAILABLE; ungroup OK. Touch drag needs a check on the real shop tablet

## [3.6.0] — 2026-06-13 — Docket/Receipt Split + Merged Sales Tab

### Changed
- **FRONT/KITCHEN auto-print is now an operational docket only** — items, modifiers, notes, order #, table, cashier; **no** prices/surcharge/totals/payment. Saves thermal paper since most customers don't take a receipt. `buildLines` in `src/lib/printer.js` no longer appends the financial block
- **Order detail buttons**: renamed **Reprint → "Reprint Docket"** and added a **"🧾 Print Receipt"** button, in both the NavBar Orders page ([OrderHistoryTable.js](src/components/admin/reports/OrderHistoryTable.js)) and the admin Sales detail modal ([SalesOrderDetailModal.js](src/components/admin/sales/SalesOrderDetailModal.js))
- **Merged admin tabs**: **Reports + Sales + Online Orders** are now one **Sales** tab (`/admin/sales`) with sub-tabs **History · Reports · Online Orders**. `/admin/reports` and `/admin/online-orders` redirect to the right sub-tab; sidebar shows a single Sales entry

### Added
- **On-demand receipt** — new `buildReceiptLines` + `printReceipt` in `src/lib/printer.js` and `POST /api/orders/[id]/receipt`. Full receipt: all items with per-line prices, subtotal, surcharge, discount, loyalty, tip, TOTAL, payment, change, "Thank you!"
- The previously-unused **`printReceipts`** PrinterConfig flag is now meaningful: receipts print to whichever printer has it enabled (fallback FRONT)
- View components `ReportsView` / `SalesHistoryView` / `OnlineOrdersView` (extracted from the old pages) feed the merged tab

### Verified
- Fake-printer test (order #43): FRONT/KITCHEN dockets print items only (no totals); the receipt prints all items with prices, Subtotal $15.00, Surcharge (1.5%) +$0.22, TOTAL $15.22, Payment, and routed to the FRONT printer (printReceipts=true)

## [3.5.1] — 2026-06-12 — Terminal Setup Docs

### Docs
- Added `docs/terminal-setup.md` — guide for connecting a real Stripe Terminal smart reader (S700 / WisePOS E): prerequisites, creating a Location, registering the reader, configuring it as `COUNTER` in Admin → Payments, go-live notes, and troubleshooting. Linked from `README.md` and the Payment Terminal section of `CLAUDE.md`
- Rewrote `README.md` from the create-next-app boilerplate into a real project overview: tech stack, features, roles, prerequisites, `.env` setup (incl. the now-required `JWT_SECRET`), running (dev/prod launchers), npm + dev/test scripts, documentation links, and the deployment model

## [3.5.0] — 2026-06-12 — Security Hardening

### Security
- **Fixed customer PII exposure via order tracking (high).** `GET /api/orders/track/[id]` previously took the **sequential order id** and returned the order `note`, which holds `Pickup: <name> | <phone>` — so anyone could enumerate `/api/orders/track/1,2,3…` over the internet and harvest every online customer's name + phone. Tracking now looks up by a new **unguessable `Order.trackingToken`** (UUID) and the route no longer returns the raw `note`; it returns only a parsed `pickupName` (phone never leaves the server). Migration `20260612120000_add_order_tracking_token`. Online order creation mints the token; success pages redirect to `/order/track/<token>`
- **Session cookie is now `httpOnly` (medium).** `manager_session` (the 4h MANAGER/ADMIN JWT) was set with `httpOnly: false`, readable by any script. It's now `httpOnly`; the client gets role/name from `GET /api/auth/manager` instead of reading/decoding the cookie ([admin/layout.js](src/app/admin/layout.js), [NavBar.js](src/components/shared/NavBar.js)). No `secure` flag — the in-shop POS is served over plain HTTP on the LAN
- **Removed hardcoded JWT fallback secret (medium).** `src/lib/auth.js` no longer falls back to a known in-repo signing key; it now **throws at startup if `JWT_SECRET` is unset**. A real `JWT_SECRET` was added to `.env` (the app had been running on the default). Existing manager sessions are invalidated — managers re-enter their PIN

### Known / deferred (low, from the same audit)
- Public customer register/lookup still reveals whether a phone is registered (enumeration oracle); rate-limited. PIN auth has no per-account lockout (LAN-only). Tracked for a later pass

## [3.4.2] — 2026-06-12 — Merge Payments Admin Tabs

### Changed
- **Combined the "Payment Terminal" and "Card Surcharge" admin tabs into one "Payments" tab** (`/admin/terminal`): reader config, flat card surcharge, and blocked brands now live on a single page with separate Save actions (reader vs surcharge & brands). Sidebar shows one **Payments** entry; `/admin/payments` now redirects to `/admin/terminal`

## [3.4.1] — 2026-06-12 — Surcharge Visibility + Amex Block Test

### Added
- **`GET /api/payment-settings/public`** — minimal, display-only surcharge (`cardSurchargeType`, `cardSurchargeValue`); no blocked brands, no auth. Whitelisted in the zone proxy (`src/proxy.js`) so the public customer page can read it. Full `GET /api/payment-settings` stays LAN-only
- **Customer order page now shows the surcharge** ([order/page.js](src/app/order/page.js)) — the "Your Order" drawer breaks down **Subtotal / Card surcharge (N%) / Total** so the figure matches the Stripe Checkout page. No surcharge → single Total row as before (display-only; server still recomputes at checkout)
- **`scripts/test-amex-block.js`** — dev/test script (test mode) that presents an **Amex** `card_present` test card and drives the real `/api/terminal/finalize` to prove a blocked brand is voided. Temporarily blocks `amex` and restores prior settings. Verified: brand `amex` → finalize `blocked` → PaymentIntent `canceled`, no charge

## [3.4.0] — 2026-06-12 — Card Surcharge & Brand Blocking

### Added
- **`PaymentSettings` model** (singleton, id=1): `cardSurchargeType` (`PERCENT`/`FIXED`), `cardSurchargeValue`, `blockedBrands` (canonical keys: `visa`, `mastercard`, `amex`, `discover`). Migration `20260612000000_add_payment_settings`
- **`src/lib/paymentSettings.js`** — settings loader + helpers. `CARD_BRANDS` maps each brand to its in-person value (`card_present.brand`) and online value (`brands_blocked`). `computeCardSurcharge`, `blockedBrandsOnline`, `isBrandBlocked`, `brandLabel`
- **`GET/PATCH /api/payment-settings`** — GET is LAN-readable (POS needs the surcharge); PATCH requires ADMIN
- **Admin page `/admin/payments`** ("Card Surcharge") — ADMIN-only UI to set the flat card surcharge (percent or fixed) and tick blocked brands
- **`POST /api/terminal/finalize`** — called once the reader authorizes (PaymentIntent → `requires_capture`); reads the real `card_present.brand`, voids the hold if the brand is blocked (no charge), otherwise captures. Catches phone wallets where staff can't see the card

### Changed
- **In-person card charges now use manual capture** (`/api/terminal/charge`) so the brand can be inspected before money moves. POS polling calls `/api/terminal/finalize` on authorization, then creates the order only on a successful capture; a blocked brand shows "… is not accepted here" with no charge
- **Flat card surcharge auto-applies on the POS Card path** ([CheckoutModal.js](src/components/pos/CheckoutModal.js)) — selecting Card fills the existing surcharge fields from config; switching away clears it (manual surcharges untouched)
- **Online checkout** ([orders/online/route.js](src/app/api/orders/online/route.js)) — adds the flat surcharge as a server-computed line item (online is always card) and passes `payment_method_options.card.restrictions.brands_blocked` so Stripe rejects blocked brands (incl. Apple/Google Pay/Link) before payment

### Split payments
- **Split card legs are now fully terminal-integrated.** Each `CARD` person in a split charges the Stripe reader for their share (incl. the flat surcharge), with brand blocking enforced per leg via the shared `runTerminalCharge` → `/api/terminal/finalize` flow. Previously card legs were recorded manually without charging
- Surcharge in a split is **per card leg** (cash legs unaffected); the aggregate is stored on the order. Top-level auto-surcharge is suppressed while in split mode
- The order stores **all** card-leg PaymentIntents comma-joined in `Order.paymentIntentId`; `POST /api/orders` verification and the refund route both split on `,` and process each — so multi-leg refunds work
- `SplitPersonStep` now remounts per person (`key`), shows a per-leg card processing/cancel state, and falls back to manual recording when no reader is configured

### Notes
- Stripe has no native surcharging; it's computed in-app. Brand blocking is native online only — in person it's enforced via the manual-capture + finalize flow

## [3.3.2] — 2026-06-11 — Production Deployment: Self-Hosted + Cloudflare Tunnel

### Deployment
- **Moved off Railway to self-hosting on the shop PC.** The app + local PostgreSQL run on the LAN; only the customer surface is exposed to the internet. Railway is no longer used (its data was disposable)
- **Custom domain `dreamy-cafe.com`** (registered via Cloudflare Registrar) now serves the public customer site at `dreamy-cafe.com/order` through a **named Cloudflare Tunnel** (`dreamycafe`, id `295e5188-…`) pointing at `http://localhost:3000`
- **Zone lockdown activated** — `PUBLIC_ZONE_SECRET` set in `.env` and a matching **Cloudflare Transform Rule** SETs `x-dreamy-zone` on `Hostname equals dreamy-cafe.com`. Verified end-to-end: `/order` → 200; `/pos`, `/admin` → 307 redirect to `/order`; `/api/orders`, `/api/users/list` → 404 over the public domain, while LAN access is unchanged
- Customer orders now write to **local Postgres** (`DATABASE_URL=…@localhost:5432/dreamycafe`); the same DB the in-shop POS reads and the LAN printers serve

### Changed
- **Desktop launchers split into two** — `start-pos.bat` (Production: `npm run build && npm run start`) and `start-pos-dev.bat` (Dev: `npm run dev`); both also start `cloudflared tunnel run dreamycafe` and open the browser. Two desktop shortcuts: "DreamyCafe POS" and "DreamyCafe POS (Dev)". Run only one at a time (shared port 3000 + tunnel). Production mode also eliminates the dev-only `_next/webpack-hmr` websocket errors seen in the tunnel log

### Verified
- **Stripe webhook end-to-end** — `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` updated to the `dreamy-cafe.com` endpoint's signing secret; a signed `checkout.session.completed` POST to `https://dreamy-cafe.com/api/webhooks/stripe` returns `200 {received:true}` (signature verification passes through tunnel + zone allowlist)
- **Live online order confirmed** — a real test order on `dreamy-cafe.com/order` (test card, AUD 7.00) completed the full flow: `checkout.session.completed` delivered, order fulfilled and recorded. Online ordering is live
- **Printing verified without hardware** — pointed the FRONT/KITCHEN printer config at `127.0.0.1` and confirmed dockets (test print + order dockets) reach a local fake ESC/POS receiver with correct content/formatting
- **Payment terminal verified without hardware** (Stripe simulated WisePOS E reader): admin $1.00 test charge → succeeded; card POS order (AUD 4.50) → succeeded; **declined card** (test card `4000000000000002`) → reader action `failed` / PaymentIntent `requires_payment_method`, so no order is created (POS shows the error)

### Tooling (dev/test, kept in repo)
- **`fake-printer.js`** — TCP listener on port 9100 that prints received ESC/POS dockets to the console, for testing printing without a physical printer
- **`scripts/test-declined-charge.js`** — runs the Terminal charge flow but presents a declining test card, to verify the decline path (complements the existing `scripts/create-simulated-reader.js`)

### Notes / Pending
- Tunnel currently runs via the desktop button (not the `cloudflared` Windows service) — use one or the other, never both, to avoid duplicate-connection churn
- For reboot-resilience consider running the app under pm2 and `cloudflared service install`; also set Windows power/sleep to "Never" so online orders keep flowing
- Stripe webhook endpoint must point at `https://dreamy-cafe.com/api/webhooks/stripe` with its own `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET`
- Data now lives only on the shop PC — set up a periodic `pg_dump` backup of `dreamycafe`

---

## [3.3.1] — 2026-06-07 — Online Order Fulfillment Resilience

### Fixed
- **Online order stuck on "confirming…" when the webhook doesn't arrive** (e.g. local dev without the Stripe CLI, or a delayed/missed event). The success page only advanced on the webhook flipping the order to `PENDING`

### Added
- **`src/lib/fulfillment.js`** — single idempotent `fulfillPaidOrder()` that flips `AWAITING_PAYMENT → PENDING` via an atomic conditional update, then runs side effects (loyalty stamps, voucher usage, stock, printing) exactly once regardless of which caller wins
- **Success-poll fallback** — `GET /api/orders/online?sessionId=` now verifies `payment_status` directly with Stripe and fulfills the order if paid, so confirmation no longer depends solely on the webhook (also makes local online-order testing work without the Stripe CLI)

### Changed
- `POST /api/webhooks/stripe` now delegates to `fulfillPaidOrder()` (same logic, shared with the fallback)

---

## [3.3.0] — 2026-06-07 — In-Person Card Payments (Stripe Terminal)

### Added
- **Stripe Terminal integration (server-driven)** — the POS can now drive a physical smart reader (S700 / WisePOS E) for in-person card payments. The server creates a PaymentIntent and tells the reader to process it; card data never touches the app (PCI scope stays minimal)
- **`src/lib/terminal.js`** — Stripe Terminal helpers (`getStripe`, `resolveReaderId`, `isTestMode`, `currency`); reader resolved from `TerminalReader` config or `STRIPE_TERMINAL_READER_ID` env
- **`POST /api/terminal/charge`** — creates a `card_present` PaymentIntent and calls `processPaymentIntent` on the reader; in test mode it simulates the tap via Stripe test helpers
- **`GET /api/terminal/status?paymentIntentId=`** — polls PaymentIntent status/last error
- **`POST /api/terminal/cancel`** — cancels the in-flight reader action + PaymentIntent
- **`GET/POST /api/terminal/readers`** — read config + (manager, `?stripe=1`) list live Stripe readers; POST upserts the `COUNTER` reader config (manager only)
- **`/admin/terminal`** — admin page to set the reader label / Stripe reader ID, enable it, and run a $1.00 test charge; added "Payment Terminal" to the admin sidebar
- **CheckoutModal card flow** — when a terminal is configured, tapping "Card" → Confirm starts the charge, shows a "Tap or insert card" overlay, polls for the result, and only creates the order on success (with retry/cancel). Cash flow unchanged; split-payment card legs remain manual for now
- **Real card refunds** — `POST /api/orders/[id]/refund` now issues a Stripe refund when the order has a `paymentIntentId` (online or terminal); refusal blocks the status change
- **Schema** — `Order.paymentIntentId`, new `TerminalReader` model; migration `20260607130000_payment_terminal`

### Security
- `POST /api/orders` verifies the Stripe PaymentIntent is `succeeded`/`requires_capture` before recording a card order as paid (rejects with 402 otherwise); manual CARD marking without a PaymentIntent still works on the trusted LAN

---

## [3.2.0] — 2026-06-07 — Public/Private Zone Split, Online Loyalty & Vouchers

### Security
- **Zone-aware route protection** (`src/lib/zone.js` + `src/proxy.js`) — requests are now classified as **public** (internet, via tunnel) or **LAN** (trusted shop network). Public requests are restricted to a strict customer allowlist (`/order*`, `/onlineorder*`, `/loyalty`, read-only menu, online-order create/track, Stripe webhook, customer register/lookup, voucher validate); every staff/admin page and API returns 404/redirect from the public zone. LAN behavior is unchanged (admin still gated by `manager_session` JWT)
- A public request is identified by the `x-dreamy-zone` header matching `PUBLIC_ZONE_SECRET`. The tunnel/edge (e.g. a Cloudflare Transform Rule) must **SET** this header on the public hostname so it cannot be forged or stripped. If `PUBLIC_ZONE_SECRET` is unset (local dev), everything is treated as LAN
- **`GET /api/orders/track/[id]`** — new public, read-only tracking endpoint returning only `{ id, status, total, note, items }`. Customer track pages (`/order`, `/onlineorder`) now use it instead of `/api/orders/[id]`, which exposed the full order and allowed edits via PATCH
- **`/api/customers` hardened** — in the public zone, GET requires a phone, is rate-limited, returns only stamp-card fields (no enumeration of the full list/record); POST (register) is rate-limited and validates input
- **In-memory rate limiter** (`src/lib/ratelimit.js`) applied to public customer lookup/register and voucher validation

### Added
- **Online orders now earn loyalty stamps** — computed **server-side** in the Stripe webhook from `loyaltyEnabled` items on confirmed payment only, then `Customer.stampsCollected` and `Order.stampEarned` are updated. Client-supplied stamp counts are never trusted on public paths
- **Voucher system** — new `Voucher` model (`PERCENT` / `FIXED` / `FREE_ITEM`, with `expiresAt`, `usageLimit`, `timesUsed`, `minSubtotal`, optional `customerId`) and `Order.voucherId` relation
- **`POST /api/vouchers/validate`** — public, rate-limited endpoint that validates a code and returns the server-computed discount
- **Voucher application in online checkout** — `POST /api/orders/online` accepts `voucherCode`, re-validates server-side, stores `discountAmount`/`voucherId`, and applies the discount to the Stripe charge via a one-time coupon; the webhook increments `Voucher.timesUsed` on payment
- **Migration** `20260607120000_zone_loyalty_vouchers`
- **`DEPLOY.md`** — production deployment guide: LAN host setup, ethernet printers, Cloudflare Transform Rule (`x-dreamy-zone`), Cloudflare Tunnel, Stripe webhook, and a verification checklist

---

- **`/admin/online-orders`** — new admin analytics page for online pickup orders: date selector, 4 summary cards (Total Orders, Completed Revenue, Avg Order Value, Active Now), status breakdown pills, and a full orders table showing order #, time, customer name + phone (parsed from order note), item emojis, total, and status badge

## [3.1.0] — 2026-06-06 — Online Pickup Orders with Stripe Payment

### Added
- **`/order`** — public customer-facing online ordering page: category tabs, product grid, per-product size/modifier/notes modal, cart drawer, and name + phone collection; no login required
- **`/order/success`** — post-payment landing page that polls until the Stripe webhook fires and the order is confirmed, then auto-redirects to the tracker
- **`/order/track/[id]`** — live order status tracker for customers; polls every 10 s; shows 4-step progress (Received → Preparing → Ready → Done) with a green "ready for pickup" banner
- **`POST /api/orders/online`** — creates a pre-order with `status: "AWAITING_PAYMENT"` then opens a Stripe Checkout Session; returns `checkoutUrl`
- **`GET /api/orders/online?sessionId=`** — looks up an order by Stripe session ID (used by `/order/success` to detect payment confirmation)
- **`POST /api/webhooks/stripe`** — receives `checkout.session.completed` events, verifies Stripe signature from raw body, promotes the order to `PENDING`, decrements stock, and triggers docket printing
- **`ConditionalNavBar`** component — hides the internal POS NavBar on all `/order/*` routes so customers see a clean storefront UI
- **`Order.source`** field (`"POS"` default / `"ONLINE"`) — all in-store orders remain `"POS"` automatically; no existing code or data affected
- **`Order.stripeSessionId`** field — unique nullable string linking an order to its Stripe Checkout Session
- **"ONLINE PICKUP" badge** on `OrderCard` — blue badge shown on kitchen/front displays when `order.source === "ONLINE"` so staff know the customer is picking up

### Dependencies
- `stripe` npm package added (server-side SDK)
- Requires three `.env` variables: `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY`, `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, `STRIPE_CURRENCY` (defaults to `"aud"`)

- **`PATCH /api/reservations/[id]`** — auth gate removed; staff can now confirm/cancel/complete reservations directly from the POS without a manager session
- **`GET /api/reservations`** — added `?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD` range filter for analytics queries
