## [3.0.0] — 2026-06-05 — Reservation System

### Added
- **Reservation model** — new `Reservation` table in the database with fields: guest name, phone, scheduled date/time, party size, note, and status (`PENDING`, `CONFIRMED`, `COMPLETED`, `CANCELLED`, `NO_SHOW`)
- **`GET /api/reservations`** — list reservations, filterable by date (`?date=YYYY-MM-DD`) and status
- **`POST /api/reservations`** — create a reservation (no auth required; staff-accessible)
- **`DELETE /api/reservations/[id]`** — delete a reservation (MANAGER/ADMIN only)
- **Staff page `/reservations`** — staff can browse reservations by day and add new ones via a form modal (guest name, date, time, party size, phone, note)
- **Sidebar "Reservations" link** — added between Cash and Loyalty in the admin navigation
- **POS "Reservations" toolbar button** — opens an inline modal on the POS page so staff can browse today's reservations and add new ones without leaving the POS

---

## [2.9.7] — 2026-06-04 — Fix Cashier Persisting Without Clock-In

### Fixed
- **Cashier shown without clocking in** — `CashierContext` now verifies the localStorage-restored user is currently clocked in on startup; if not, the stored session is cleared
- **`GET /api/users/list?clockedIn=1` logic was inverted** — the filter was keeping users who had clocked OUT (`.length > 0`) instead of those who hadn't (`.length === 0`); also the OUT record timestamp filter was `gte: now` (future) instead of `lte: now` (past); both corrected

---

## [2.9.6] — 2026-06-04 — Table History: 24h Limit + Order Timestamps

### Changed
- Table history now only shows orders from the **last 24 hours** (previously showed all-time history)
- Each order card (both active and served) now displays the **time**, **date**, and **cashier name** who placed the order

---

## [2.9.5] — 2026-06-02 — Money In/Out PIN Gate + Cash Movement Tracking

### Added
- **PIN gate on In/Out button** — clicking "In/Out" in the POS toolbar now prompts a Manager/Admin PIN before opening the Money In/Out modal; only MANAGER and ADMIN roles can access it
- `CashMovement.userId` field (nullable FK → User) added to the database; migration `add_cash_movement_user` applied
- `POST /api/cash/movements` now accepts `userId` and stores it; response includes the `user` relation
- `GET /api/cash/movements` now supports `?date=YYYY-MM-DD` query param to fetch all movements for a given day (without the param, still returns current session movements)
- **Cash Movements section** in Admin → Cash Drawer page: date picker + table showing time, IN/OUT badge, who recorded it, note, and signed amount; net total shown in footer
- MoneyInOutModal shows "Recording as {name}" when a verified user is present, and includes their name in the success screen

### Changed
- `ManagerPinModal.onSuccess` now passes `{ id, name, role }` to the callback — existing callers that ignore the argument are unaffected
- `/api/auth/manager` POST response now includes `id` and `role` alongside `name`

---

## [2.9.4] — 2026-06-01 — Order Discount/Surcharge from Cart Total

### Added
- **Order Adjustment Modal** — clicking the "Total" row in the cart opens a modal to apply an order-level discount ($) and/or surcharge (% or flat $)
- `OrderAdjustmentModal` (`src/components/pos/OrderAdjustmentModal.js`): live preview of adjusted total; "Clear" button resets both fields; adjustment state persists until order is confirmed or manually cleared
- CartSummary now shows a breakdown (Subtotal / Discount / Surcharge / Total) when adjustments are active; the Total row is the clickable trigger
- Order-level discount and surcharge are pre-populated in CheckoutModal when it opens, so the checkout grand total is consistent
- Adjustments are reset automatically after an order is confirmed

---

## [2.9.3] — 2026-06-01 — Cart Item Edit Modal

### Added
- **Cart item edit modal** — tapping the name/info area of any cart item opens a full edit modal with General and Options tabs
- General tab: quantity stepper (min 1), unit price + live line total, inline "Add Adjustment" section (amount + reason), note textarea
- Options tab: read-only list of modifiers and size for the item
- "Remove" button (red, top-right) removes the item and closes the modal
- "Apply Changes" applies all edits (qty, note, adjustment) in one shot
- `updateItem(cartItemId, patches)` added to `useCart` hook — general-purpose field patcher used by the modal
- Inline `−`/`+` quantity buttons on cart items still work independently without opening the modal

### Removed
- `PriceAdjustmentModal` removed from `Cart.js` (its functionality is absorbed by the new edit modal)
- Small `±` price-adjust button removed from `CartItem` (replaced by the edit modal's adjustment section)

## [2.9.2] — 2026-06-01 — Switch User Button on POS

### Added
- **Switch User button** in the bottom-left of the POS category sidebar — shows a person icon with the current cashier's initials badge and "Switch User" label
- `SwitchUserModal` component (`src/components/pos/SwitchUserModal.js`): step 1 shows all active users in a grid with initials avatars; step 2 shows a 4-digit PIN keypad after selecting a user; on success, updates the cashier context
- `POST /api/auth/verify-pin` endpoint — validates a PIN for any active user (any role) and returns user info without creating a manager JWT session; used exclusively for cashier switching

## [2.9.1] — 2026-05-31 — Category Default Add-ons

### Added
- **Category-level modifiers** — Admins can define default add-ons per category (e.g. all Coffee products get "Oat milk", "Extra shot"; all Food products get "Bacon", "Extra avocado"). Configured in Menu Management → Edit category → Default Add-ons section.
- `CategoryModifier` model: `id`, `categoryId`, `name`, `price`, `available`
- API: `GET/POST /api/categories/[id]/modifiers`, `PUT/DELETE /api/categories/[id]/modifiers/[modId]`
- `CategoryModifierManager` component rendered when editing a category (ADMIN only)
- `GET /api/products` now includes `category.modifiers` so POS receives them without extra fetches
- POS ProductOptionsModal merges product-specific and category-level modifiers into one Add-ons section; each modifier tagged with `source: 'product' | 'category'` so the order API snapshots to the correct FK

### Fixed
- `OrderItemModifier.modifierId` is now nullable; added `categoryModifierId` (also nullable) to support both modifier sources
- ProductOptionsModal: `selectedModifiers`, `selectedSize`, and `notes` now reset correctly when a new product is opened (fixes carry-over between product selections)

---

## [2.9.0] — 2026-05-31 — Product Modifiers, Refunds, Tip, Surcharge, Price Adjustments, Sales History, Printer Config

### Added
- **Product Modifiers (Add-ons)** — Admin can configure modifiers per product (e.g. "Avocado +$4", "Extra Shot +$0.50"). At POS, staff select modifiers as pill buttons in the options modal. Modifier names and prices snapshot to `OrderItemModifier` records at order time. Modifiers print on kitchen/front dockets under each item.
- **Refund Processing** — Completed orders can be refunded from Order History or the new Sales page. Requires a refund note. Order status changes to `REFUNDED` (purple badge). Endpoint: `POST /api/orders/[id]/refund` (MANAGER+ required).
- **Tip Recording** — Checkout modal has a Tip section with $0/$1/$2/$5 preset buttons and custom input. Tip is included in the grand total and printed on receipts.
- **Order-level Surcharge & Discount** — Checkout modal has Surcharge (% or flat $) and Manual Discount (flat $) inputs. Both stack with loyalty rewards. Shown in the grand total breakdown at checkout and in order details.
- **Item-level Price Adjustment** — Each cart item has a `±` button that opens a modal to add a positive or negative adjustment with an optional note (e.g. "Staff meal −$2"). Shown in cart and checkout summary.
- **Sales History Page** — New `/admin/sales` page with date range quick-filters (Today/Yesterday/7 Days/Custom), search by order number or payment method, status filter, and full order detail modal with refund capability.
- **Network Printer Configuration** — New `/admin/printers` page (ADMIN only) to configure FRONT and KITCHEN thermal printers via UI (IP, port, toggles for dockets/receipts/drawer). `PrinterConfig` model persists settings. `printer.js` now reads from DB with env-var fallback. Test print button per printer.

### Changed
- `Order` model: added `tipAmount`, `surchargeType`, `surchargeValue`, `surchargeAmount`, `manualDiscount`, `refundNote` fields
- `OrderItem` model: added `priceAdjustment`, `priceAdjustmentNote`, and `modifiers` relation
- `Product` model: added `modifiers` relation
- Total calculation in `POST /api/orders` now includes surcharge, discounts, tip, modifier prices, and item adjustments
- `GET /api/orders` and `GET /api/sales` now include `OrderItemModifier` in items
- Sidebar: added Sales and Printers links
- Order status constants: added `REFUNDED`

---

## [2.8.3] — 2026-05-31 — Fix cashier picker and payroll hour inflation

### Fixed
- **Cashier picker "on shift" check was wrong** — clock-in modal records the full shift (IN + OUT) upfront in one submission; the correct check is whether current time falls between a user's IN and OUT timestamps today (`IN.timestamp <= now <= OUT.timestamp`), not whether their last record type is `IN`
- **Payroll inflated hours from unpaired records** — old test data had IN records without matching OUTs; those are now skipped instead of counting up to the current time

---

## [2.8.2] — 2026-05-30 — Cashier Picker shows clocked-in staff only

### Changed
- **"Who is taking this order?"** modal now only lists staff who are currently clocked in (most recent ClockRecord is type `IN`); shows "No staff currently clocked in." when the list is empty
- **`GET /api/users/list?clockedIn=1`** — new query param; fetches ACTIVE users whose latest clock record is `IN`

---

## [2.8.1] — 2026-05-30 — Fix Admin session context not refreshing

### Fixed
- **Admin/Manager context not updating on role switch** — `AdminSessionContext` was loaded once on layout mount; switching between Manager and Admin via NavBar buttons would not update the context, so role-gated features (Payroll tab, stock controls) remained in the previous role's state
- **Fix**: NavBar dispatches a `session-changed` custom event on successful login; `AdminLayout` listens for it and re-fetches the session

---

## [2.8.0] — 2026-05-30 — User Account Approval Workflow

### Added
- **`status` field on `User` model** — `"ACTIVE"` (default) or `"PENDING"`; migration `20260530130908_add_user_status`
- **Pending Approvals section** in Users page — shows accounts awaiting approval with amber highlight; Admin sees Approve / Decline buttons; Manager sees "Awaiting admin approval" label
- **Approval flow**: Admin approves via `PATCH /api/users/[id]` with `{ status: 'ACTIVE' }`; decline calls DELETE

### Changed
- **`POST /api/users`** — Manager-created accounts are set to `status: 'PENDING'`; Admin-created accounts go directly to `status: 'ACTIVE'`; Manager cannot create ADMIN-role accounts (restricted to STAFF/MANAGER)
- **`DELETE /api/users/[id]`** — Admin-only (was MANAGER or ADMIN)
- **`PATCH /api/users/[id]`** — Admin can set `status` field; Manager cannot; Manager cannot assign ADMIN role
- **`GET /api/users/list`** — filtered to `status: 'ACTIVE'` only so PENDING users cannot appear in PIN login or clock-in modals
- **Users page form** — role options filtered by session role (Manager sees STAFF/MANAGER; Admin sees all three); submit button label changes to "Submit for Approval" when Manager is creating a new account

---

## [2.7.0] — 2026-05-30 — Admin (Owner) Role & Payroll

### Added
- **ADMIN role** — new owner-level role above MANAGER; created via Users page (role dropdown now includes ADMIN)
- **"Admin" button in NavBar** — dedicated button for owner login; opens "Owner Access" PIN modal showing only ADMIN users; navigates to `/admin/menu` on success
- **"Manager" button in NavBar** — renamed from "Admin"; opens "Manager Access" PIN modal showing only MANAGER users
- **`AdminSessionContext`** — exported from `src/app/admin/layout.js`; provides `{ id, name, role }` to all admin child pages so they can gate features without each page fetching the session independently
- **Payroll tab in Timesheets** — visible to ADMIN only; shows per-staff hours summary with Today / This Week presets, custom date range, day-by-day columns, total hours per person, column totals footer, and print button
- **`GET /api/reports/payroll?from=&to=`** — ADMIN-only endpoint; pairs IN/OUT clock records per user per day, calculates hours (unpaired IN counts up to now), returns `[{ id, name, role, days, totalHours }]`

### Changed
- **`src/proxy.js`** — `/admin/*` now allows both MANAGER and ADMIN roles
- **`POST /api/auth/manager`** — accepts users with MANAGER or ADMIN role
- **`GET /api/auth/manager`** — returns session for both MANAGER and ADMIN roles
- **`ManagerPinModal`** — accepts optional `role` prop; filters user list by role when provided; title changes to "Owner Access" for ADMIN
- **Stock page** — MANAGER sees quantity + threshold inputs only (name/unit as read-only text, no Add Item or delete buttons); ADMIN has full control
- **`POST /api/stock-items`** — ADMIN role required (was unauthenticated)
- **`DELETE /api/stock-items/[id]`** — ADMIN role required
- **`PATCH /api/stock-items/[id]`** — MANAGER or ADMIN role required

## [2.6.0] — 2026-05-30 — Independent Stock Count

### Changed
- **Stock Count completely decoupled from POS menu** — stock items are now raw ingredients/supplies (e.g. "Coffee beans — 50 bags") with no relation to POS products
- Stock page reworked: flat table with Item Name, Unit, Quantity, Alert Below, Status columns; no category grouping; no "Sold Today" column
- "Order Report" renamed to "Stock Report" — shows Item, Unit, Quantity, Status (printable)

### Added
- **`StockItem` model** — new Prisma model: `id, name, unit, quantity, lowStockThreshold, createdAt`; independent of `Product`
- **`GET/POST /api/stock-items`** — list all items (ordered by name) and create new items
- **`PATCH/DELETE /api/stock-items/[id]`** — update fields or delete an item
- **Add Item inline row** — "+ Add Item" button reveals a new row at the bottom of the table with inputs for name, unit, quantity, threshold; "Add" button creates it via POST
- **Delete per row** — × button removes a stock item immediately
- **`GET /api/stock?alerts=1`** sidebar alerts now query `StockItem` instead of `Product`

### Schema
- Migration `20260530102742_add_stock_item_model`: added `StockItem` table

## [2.5.1] — 2026-05-30 — Fix loyaltyEnabled Checkbox Warning

### Fixed
- `src/components/admin/menu/ProductForm.js` — `checked={form.loyaltyEnabled}` changed to `checked={form.loyaltyEnabled ?? false}` to prevent React "uncontrolled → controlled" warning when editing products whose `loyaltyEnabled` field is undefined

## [2.5.0] — 2026-05-30 — Mobile Responsive Layout

### Changed
- **NavBar** — all links now scroll horizontally on small screens; nothing is cut off
- **POS page** — cart sidebar hidden on mobile; floating "View Cart" bar appears at the bottom when items are in the cart; tapping it opens a full-screen cart drawer with full edit/checkout support; toolbar buttons show icons only on small screens
- **Admin pages** — sidebar hidden on mobile; hamburger button in the top bar opens a slide-over drawer; tapping any link closes the drawer
- **Timesheets** — shift table wraps in a horizontal scroll container so columns don't clip on narrow screens
- Category sidebar narrower on mobile (`w-20` → `w-28` on desktop)

## [2.4.1] — 2026-05-30 — POS Suspense Boundary

### Fixed
- **POS page** — wrapped `PosPageContent` in a `<Suspense>` boundary so `useSearchParams()` works correctly under Next.js 16's strict client-component rules; prevents hydration errors when the page is accessed with query params (e.g. `?managerRequired=1`)

## [2.4.0] — 2026-05-29 — Clock In: One-Step Shift Recording & Manager Edit Shift

### Changed
- **Clock In is now one-step** — staff enter username, password, start time, and end time in a single form; both the IN and OUT records are created at once; no separate clock-out needed
- `POST /api/auth/clock-inout` now accepts `startTime` and `endTime` (`HH:MM`) and creates two clock records (IN + OUT) in one request; auto-detect direction logic removed

### Added
- **Manager edit shift on Timesheets** — each shift row has an "Edit" button (visible on hover); managers can change Clock In and Clock Out times inline and save
- **Add missing clock-out** — rows showing "Still clocked in" have a "+ Out" button; managers can add a missing clock-out time
- **`PATCH /api/clock/[id]`** — manager-only endpoint to update the timestamp of any clock record
- `POST /api/clock` now accepts an optional `timestamp` ISO string in the request body

## [2.3.0] — 2026-05-29 — NavBar Declutter

### Changed
- **NavBar simplified** — shows only operational links: POS, Kitchen, Front, Tables, Orders, and a single "Admin" button
- **Admin Sidebar expanded** — all management links (Menu, Stock, Cash, Loyalty, Reports, Timesheets, Users) moved to the sidebar visible on every admin page
- **Stock alert badge moved** — low-stock indicator now appears on the Stock link in the admin Sidebar instead of the NavBar

## [2.2.0] — 2026-05-29 — POS Toolbar Improvements

### Added
- **Persistent cashier button on POS** — always-visible "Cashier ▼" button in the POS toolbar opens a staff picker; selected cashier name shown at a glance; persists in localStorage
- **Order counter on POS** — "Orders: N" displays today's order count in the toolbar; refreshes after each confirmed order
- **Money In/Out from POS** — "In/Out" button in POS toolbar opens a modal to record cash entering or leaving the till without going to the admin Cash page; linked to the open cash session; updates expectedCash automatically
- **CashMovement model** — new database table tracks per-session money in/out entries with type, amount, note, and timestamp
- **Cash session expectedCash now includes movements** — the Cash drawer page correctly factors in all money-in and money-out entries when calculating expected cash

## [2.1.0] — 2026-05-29 — Account System Redesign

### Changed
- **Removed login page** — app opens directly to POS without any authentication; no session cookie required for staff use
- **Removed ADMIN role** — only STAFF and MANAGER roles; existing ADMIN users must be migrated to MANAGER via `node prisma/migrate-admin-to-manager.js`
- **Manager PIN access** — navigating to any `/admin/*` route shows a PIN prompt overlay; manager enters their PIN to unlock for a 4-hour session (`manager_session` cookie); "Lock" button in NavBar clears it
- **Clock In/Out modal** — "Clock In/Out" button in NavBar opens a modal where any staff or manager enters their username + password to record shift start/end; auto-detects direction (IN if last record was OUT, OUT if last record was IN)
- **Cashier picker in checkout** — Checkout modal now has a "Cashier" field; tapping it shows a staff list to pick who is taking the order; selection persists in localStorage; cashier name saved on order record
- **Cashier name on receipt** — front docket now prints "Cashier: [Name]" when a cashier is selected on the order
- **User management now MANAGER-accessible** — was previously ADMIN-only; any manager with an active session can manage staff accounts
- **Seed updated** — manager seed user now includes a default PIN (`1234`); admin seed user removed

### Removed
- Login page (`/login`)
- `ADMIN` role from UI and API (STAFF and MANAGER only)
- Auto clock-in/clock-out on login/logout
- Staff PIN quick-switch modal (replaced by cashier picker in checkout)
- Auth API routes: `/api/auth/login`, `/api/auth/logout`, `/api/auth/me`, `/api/auth/pin-login`

## [2.0.0] — 2026-05-29 — Major Feature Expansion

### Added
- **Low stock auto-decrement** — product stock decrements automatically on every order; red dot badge on the Stock nav link when any product is at or below its threshold
- **Custom per-product stock threshold** — "Alert Below" column in Stock page; each product has its own low-stock threshold (default 5)
- **Hourly sales heatmap** — 24-bar chart on Reports page showing revenue or order count per hour with peak hour highlighted; toggle $/# view
- **Category revenue breakdown** — horizontal bar chart on Reports page showing each category's revenue share
- **Staff activity report** — Reports page shows per-staff order count and revenue for the selected day
- **Cash drawer reconciliation** — `/admin/cash`: manager opens a daily session with an opening float, system tracks cash sales live, close till by entering counted cash, shows expected vs actual with variance; 14-day history
- **Staff PIN quick-switch** — staff log in once with password (clocking in); a "Switch" button on POS opens a PIN pad to switch the active order-taker without full logout
- **Clock-in / Clock-out** — automatic clock-in on login, clock-out on logout, clock events also recorded on PIN switches
- **Timesheets page** — `/admin/timesheets`: date-picker view of all clock-in/out events per staff with session duration and daily totals
- **Shift log** — every order now records which staff member placed it; visible as a "Staff" column in Order History
- **Loyalty stamp card** — `/admin/customers`: customers register by phone; earn 1 stamp per order containing loyalty-enabled products; 9 stamps = 1 free coffee; redeem at checkout with phone lookup; progress shown as a ☕ stamp row
- **Loyalty flag on products** — "Count toward stamp card" checkbox in Menu Management product form
- **PIN management** — set/update a 4–6 digit PIN per user in `/admin/users`

### Changed
- Schema: added `pin`, `lowStockThreshold`, `loyaltyEnabled`, `userId`, `customerId`, `stampEarned`, `freeItemRedeemed`, `discountAmount` fields; new `ClockRecord`, `CashSession`, `Customer` models

- **Split Bill** — new ✂ Split button in checkout alongside Cash/Card; staff selects 2–6 people, then each person pays their share sequentially (cash or card per person) before the order is placed; mixed-method orders are stored as `SPLIT`
- **Order editing** — ✏ Edit Order button on active orders (Pending/Preparing) in both the Orders history page and Kitchen display; staff can change item quantities, remove items, or search and add new products; total recalculates live; Completed/Ready/Cancelled orders are locked from editing

- **Confirmation warning** — selecting an occupied table shows an inline ⚠ warning with the active bill count and a checkbox staff must tick before the Confirm button unlocks, reducing accidental double-assignment

- **Table cards** are now round (like real café tables viewed from above), occupied tables are bold red with an animated pulse timer, available tables are clean white with a green dot
- **Floor background** changed to a warm off-white with a subtle dot grid pattern
- **Header** now shows live Available / Occupied counts as colored pills
- **Empty state** shown on the floor when no tables have been added yet
- **Side panel** order cards now show status badges with color-coded dots (Pending = yellow, Preparing = blue, Ready = purple, Completed = green); completed orders collapse to a compact summary row

## [1.5.0] — 2026-05-29 — Thermal Printer (Epson Network)

### Added
- **Auto-print dockets** on every new order via Epson network thermal printer (ESC/POS over TCP port 9100)
- `src/lib/printer.js` — printer utility; splits items by station (`FRONT` / `KITCHEN`) and sends separate dockets to each printer
- Front docket includes: order #, time/date, table, items, total, payment method, order note
- Kitchen docket includes: order #, time/date, table, kitchen items only (no pricing)
- Printer IPs configured via `PRINTER_FRONT_IP`, `PRINTER_KITCHEN_IP`, `PRINTER_PORT` in `.env`
- Print is fire-and-forget — a printer being offline never blocks or errors the order

## [1.0.5] — 2026-05-28 — Accessible Button Labels

### Changed
- Added `aria-label` to all buttons that used only symbols, initials, or short ambiguous text
- NavBar links: `aria-label="Go to {Page}"` + `aria-current="page"` on active link
- Category sidebar buttons: `aria-label="Show {Category} items"` + `aria-pressed` state
- Account button: `aria-label="Account menu for {Name} — {Role}"` + `aria-expanded` state
- Cart `−`/`+` buttons: `aria-label="Decrease/Increase {Product name} quantity"`
- Cart "Clear all": `aria-label="Clear all items from order"`
- Kitchen action button: `aria-label="{Action} for Order #{id}"`; Cancel text changed from "Cancel" to "Cancel order"
- ProductForm size remove button: `aria-label="Remove {Size} size"`
- Emojis marked `aria-hidden="true"` so screen readers skip them

---

## [1.0.4] — 2026-05-28 — Next.js 16 Async Params Fix

### Fixed
- All dynamic API route handlers (`[id]`) used `params.id` synchronously — Next.js 15+ requires `await params` first. Fixed in: `orders/[id]`, `tables/[id]`, `products/[id]`, `categories/[id]`, `users/[id]`
- `users/[id]/route.js` also had the `cookies()` async bug in `requireAdmin()` — fixed with `await cookies()`
- This caused kitchen "Start Preparing" / "Mark Ready" to silently fail, and table "Free Table" to do nothing

---

## [1.0.3] — 2026-05-28 — Account Details Button

### Added
- Account button pinned to the bottom of the POS category sidebar — shows user avatar (first initial + role colour), name, username, role badge, and a Sign Out button in a popup
- Disabled Next.js dev indicator (`devIndicators: false`) so it no longer appears as a confusing "N" circle in the corner

---

## [1.0.2] — 2026-05-28 — Next.js 16 Breaking Change Fixes

### Fixed
- `src/app/api/auth/me/route.js`: `cookies()` from `next/headers` is now async in Next.js 15+; changed `cookies().get()` → `(await cookies()).get()` — this was causing NavBar to receive null user and hide all navigation links
- `src/middleware.js` renamed to `src/proxy.js` and export renamed from `middleware` to `proxy` — Next.js 16 deprecated the `middleware` file convention in favour of `proxy`

---

## [1.0.1] — 2026-05-28 — Bug Fixes

### Fixed
- `src/app/admin/stock/page.js`: Extra `</div>` tag caused "Unterminated regexp literal" build error — removed stray closing tag and rewrote file with clean JSX structure
- Updated Next.js from 14.2.35 to 16.2.6 to resolve outdated framework warning

---

## [1.0.0] — 2026-05-28 — Manager Access + Stock Count

### Added
- Stock Count page (`/admin/stock`) for managers and admins: enter current stock levels, view units sold today, generate printable end-of-day order report
- `stock` field added to Product model (nullable — null means untracked)
- `GET /api/stock` endpoint: returns all products with soldToday counts aggregated from today's orders
- `PATCH /api/products/[id]` endpoint: updates stock-only without overwriting other product fields

### Changed
- Reports page (`/admin/reports`) now accessible to MANAGER role (previously ADMIN only)
- Sidebar shows "Stock Count" and "Reports" links to MANAGER role

---

## [0.9.0] — 2026-05-28 — Dynamic Size Management

### Changed
- Admin menu product form: replaced fixed Small/Medium/Large size toggles with a fully dynamic add/remove system
- Managers can add only the sizes each product needs via quick-add suggestion buttons (Small, Medium, Large, Regular, XL) or a custom label
- Sizes can be removed individually with a × button

---

## [0.8.0] — 2026-05-28 — Quick Login Buttons

### Added
- Login page now has three one-click role buttons (Staff, Manager, Admin) for quick access during development and demo

---

## [0.7.0] — 2026-05-28 — Table Management

### Added
- Floor plan page (`/tables`): draggable table cards at absolute positions, no external library
- Tables show green (AVAILABLE) or red (OCCUPIED) status
- Clicking an occupied table opens a side panel showing active orders, items, time occupied
- "Free Table" button manually marks a table available
- "Add Table" creates the next numbered table; tables can only be deleted when available
- Checkout modal: first step is a table picker (or skip for takeaway); selection links order to table
- Orders and kitchen cards show table number badge
- Table status auto-updates: OCCUPIED on order creation, AVAILABLE when last active order is completed/cancelled
- `GET/POST /api/tables`, `PATCH/DELETE /api/tables/[id]` endpoints
- Tables link added to NavBar (visible to all roles)

### Schema
- Added `Table` model with number, label, x/y position, status
- Added `tableId`, `tableNumber` fields to `Order`

---

## [0.6.0] — 2026-05-28 — Remove Tax

### Changed
- Removed 10% tax from cart, checkout modal, and order summary — payment terminal handles tax externally
- Cart shows only "Total" with no tax row

---

## [0.5.0] — 2026-05-28 — Per-Size Pricing

### Added
- Admin menu: products can have size variants with individual prices (e.g. Small $4.00, Large $5.00)
- POS product options modal: shows size buttons with price labels when sizes are configured
- Cart and orders use the size-specific unit price; same product in different sizes creates separate cart lines
- `ProductSize` model added to schema (cascades on product delete)
- `size` and `notes` fields added to `OrderItem`
- Kitchen order cards display size and notes per item

---

## [0.4.0] — 2026-05-28 — Role-Based Access Control

### Added
- `User` model with STAFF / MANAGER / ADMIN roles
- Login page (`/login`) with username + password form
- JWT session cookie (`session`, httpOnly, 1-day expiry) via `jose` + `bcryptjs`
- `POST /api/auth/login`, `POST /api/auth/logout`, `GET /api/auth/me` endpoints
- `src/middleware.js`: route protection by role — redirects unauthenticated or under-privileged users
- Staff Accounts page (`/admin/users`): CRUD for all user accounts, ADMIN only
- NavBar shows logged-in user name, role chip, and logout button; hides links by role
- Sidebar hides "Staff Accounts" from non-ADMIN roles
- Default accounts seeded: admin/admin123, manager/manager123, staff/staff123

### Route access
| Route | Roles |
|---|---|
| `/pos`, `/kitchen`, `/tables` | STAFF, MANAGER, ADMIN |
| `/admin/menu`, `/admin/stock` | MANAGER, ADMIN |
| `/admin/reports` | MANAGER, ADMIN |
| `/admin/users` | ADMIN |

---

- Migrated database from SQLite to PostgreSQL (local, database `dreamycafe`)
- Updated `prisma/schema.prisma` provider from `sqlite` to `postgresql`
- Updated `.env` `DATABASE_URL` to PostgreSQL connection string
- Deleted old SQLite migration history; ran fresh `migrate dev --name init`

- Category filter moved from horizontal tabs to a vertical left sidebar with large touch-friendly buttons
- Tapping a product opens an options modal with: size selection (S/M/L) and a special instructions notes field
- `useCart` hook updated: `addItem(product, size, notes, sizePrice)` signature; unitPrice stored per cart item

## Issues Log

### Issue #1 — npm name restriction
- **Problem**: `create-next-app` rejected `DreamyCafe` as a package name (capital letters not allowed)
- **Fix**: Scaffolded in `dreamy-cafe-temp` folder, moved files to `DreamyCafe/`, renamed `name` in `package.json` to `dreamy-cafe`
- **Status**: Resolved
